static const uint32_t CONTACT_THRESHOLD_MAX = 500000; // Beat detection (PBA) static const float THRESHOLD_FACTOR = 0.6f; static const float THRESHOLD_DECAY = 0.97f; static const uint16_t REFRACTORY_MS = 300; static const uint16_t MAX_BEAT_INTERVAL_MS = 1500; // 40 BPM static const uint16_t MIN_BEAT_INTERVAL_MS = 333; // 180 BPM static const float MIN_BEAT_AMPLITUDE = 50.0f; static const uint32_t SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS = 4000; // BPM limits static const float BPM_MIN = 40.0f; static const float BPM_MAX = 180.0f; // Averaging static const uint8_t ROLLING_AVG_SIZE = 8; static const float EMA_ALPHA = 0.3f; // Hysteresis static const float HYSTERESIS_BAND = 3.0f; static const uint32_t HYSTERESIS_LOCK_MS = 6000; static const uint32_t HYSTERESIS_INIT_MS = 4000; // State machine static const uint32_t ACQUIRING_MIN_MS = 3000; static const uint8_t MIN_BEATS_FOR_TRACKING = 5; static const uint8_t MAX_MISSED_BEATS = 3; } // ============================================================================ // [2] ADAPTIVE LED CURRENT CONTROL // ============================================================================ namespace LED_CTRL { // Target ADC range: keep signal in optimal zone // 18-bit ADC max = 262143 // Target: 25% to 75% of full scale static const uint32_t TARGET_DC_MIN = 50000; // ~19% of max static const uint32_t TARGET_DC_MAX = 180000; // ~69% of max static const uint32_t TARGET_DC_IDEAL = 100000; // ~38% of max // LED current limits (register values, 0.2mA per step) static const uint8_t LED_PA_MIN = 0x05; // 1.0 mA static const uint8_t LED_PA_MAX = 0x7F; // 25.4 mA static const uint8_t LED_PA_DEFAULT = 0x24; // 7.2 mA // Control loop timing static const uint32_t ADJUST_INTERVAL_MS = 1000; // Check every 1 second static const uint8_t STEP_SIZE_COARSE = 0x08; // 1.6 mA steps (far from target) static const uint8_t STEP_SIZE_FINE = 0x02; // 0.4 mA steps (near target) static const uint32_t COARSE_THRESHOLD = 30000; // Distance for coarse adjustment // Settling time after adjustment static const uint32_t SETTLING_MS = 200; } struct LedController { uint8_t currentRedPA; uint8_t currentIrPA; uint32_t lastAdjustTime; uint32_t lastSettleTime; bool isSettling; uint32_t adjustmentCount; bool enabled; }; LedController ledCtrl = { SENSOR_CFG::INITIAL_RED_PA, SENSOR_CFG::INITIAL_IR_PA, 0, 0, false, 0, true }; bool setLedCurrent(uint8_t redPA, uint8_t irPA) { // Clamp to limits redPA = constrain(redPA, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MIN, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MAX); irPA = constrain(irPA, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MIN, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MAX); bool success = true; if (redPA != ledCtrl.currentRedPA) { success &= writeRegister(REG::LED1_PA, redPA); if (success) ledCtrl.currentRedPA = redPA; } if (irPA != ledCtrl.currentIrPA) { success &= writeRegister(REG::LED2_PA, irPA); if (success) ledCtrl.currentIrPA = irPA; } return success; } void updateAdaptiveLedControl(uint32_t irDcLevel, uint32_t timestamp) { if (!ledCtrl.enabled) return; // Don't adjust during settling if (ledCtrl.isSettling) { if (timestamp - ledCtrl.lastSettleTime >= LED_CTRL::SETTLING_MS) { ledCtrl.isSettling = false; } return; } // Check interval if (timestamp - ledCtrl.lastAdjustTime < LED_CTRL::ADJUST_INTERVAL_MS) { return; } ledCtrl.lastAdjustTime = timestamp; // No contact — don't adjust if (irDcLevel < PROC::CONTACT_THRESHOLD_MIN) { return; } // Determine if adjustment needed bool needsAdjust = false; int8_t direction = 0; // +1 = increase, -1 = decrease uint8_t stepSize = LED_CTRL::STEP_SIZE_FINE; if (irDcLevel < LED_CTRL::TARGET_DC_MIN) { // Signal too low — increase LED current needsAdjust = true; direction = +1; uint32_t deficit = LED_CTRL::TARGET_DC_IDEAL - irDcLevel; if (deficit > LED_CTRL::COARSE_THRESHOLD) { stepSize = LED_CTRL::STEP_SIZE_COARSE; } } else if (irDcLevel > LED_CTRL::TARGET_DC_MAX) { // Signal too high (near saturation) — decrease LED current needsAdjust = true; direction = -1; uint32_t excess = irDcLevel - LED_CTRL::TARGET_DC_IDEAL; if (excess > LED_CTRL::COARSE_THRESHOLD) { stepSize = LED_CTRL::STEP_SIZE_COARSE; } } if (needsAdjust) { int16_t newRedPA = (int16_t)ledCtrl.currentRedPA + (direction * stepSize); int16_t newIrPA = (int16_t)ledCtrl.currentIrPA + (direction * stepSize); newRedPA = constrain(newRedPA, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MIN, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MAX); newIrPA = constrain(newIrPA, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MIN, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MAX); if (setLedCurrent((uint8_t)newRedPA, (uint8_t)newIrPA)) { ledCtrl.isSettling = true; ledCtrl.lastSettleTime = timestamp; ledCtrl.adjustmentCount++; Serial.print(F("[LED] Adjusted: RED=")); Serial.print(newRedPA * 0.2f, 1); Serial.print(F("mA, IR=")); Serial.print(newIrPA * 0.2f, 1); Serial.print(F("mA (DC=")); Serial.print(irDcLevel); Serial.println(F(")")); } } } // ============================================================================ // [3] NUMERIC SIGNAL QUALITY INDEX (SQI) // ============================================================================ namespace SQI { // Weight factors for composite SQI static const float W_CONTACT = 0.25f; // Contact quality static const float W_PERFUSION = 0.25f; // Perfusion index static const float W_STABILITY = 0.25f; // Beat-to-beat stability static const float W_AMPLITUDE = 0.25f; // Signal amplitude adequacy // Perfusion thresholds for scoring static const float PERF_EXCELLENT = 0.02f; // >2% perfusion static const float PERF_GOOD = 0.005f; // >0.5% static const float PERF_MINIMUM = 0.001f; // >0.1% // Amplitude thresholds static const float AMP_EXCELLENT = 500.0f; static const float AMP_GOOD = 200.0f; static const float AMP_MINIMUM = 50.0f; // Beat interval variance thresholds (ms²) static const float VAR_EXCELLENT = 400.0f; // <20ms std dev static const float VAR_GOOD = 2500.0f; // <50ms std dev static const float VAR_MAXIMUM = 10000.0f; // <100ms std dev } struct SignalQualityIndex { float contactScore; // 0.0–1.0 float perfusionScore; // 0.0–1.0 float stabilityScore; // 0.0–1.0 float amplitudeScore; // 0.0–1.0 float composite; // 0.0–1.0 (weighted combination) bool contactDetected; float perfusionRatio; float signalAmplitude; uint32_t dcLevel; // Beat interval tracking for stability float intervalVariance; float lastIntervals[8]; uint8_t intervalCount; uint8_t intervalIndex; }; SignalQualityIndex sqi = {0}; float computeScoreLinear(float value, float minimum, float good, float excellent) { if (value <= 0.0f) return 0.0f; if (value < minimum) return 0.1f; if (value < good) return 0.1f + 0.4f * (value - minimum) / (good - minimum); if (value < excellent) return 0.5f + 0.5f * (value - good) / (excellent - good); return 1.0f; } float computeScoreInverse(float value, float excellent, float good, float maximum) { if (value <= excellent) return 1.0f; if (value <= good) return 0.5f + 0.5f * (1.0f - (value - excellent) / (good - excellent)); if (value <= maximum) return 0.1f + 0.4f * (1.0f - (value - good) / (maximum - good)); return 0.0f; } void addBeatInterval(float intervalMs) { sqi.lastIntervals[sqi.intervalIndex] = intervalMs; sqi.intervalIndex = (sqi.intervalIndex + 1) % 8; if (sqi.intervalCount < 8) sqi.intervalCount++; // Compute variance if (sqi.intervalCount >= 3) { float mean = 0.0f; for (uint8_t i = 0; i < sqi.intervalCount; i++) { mean += sqi.lastIntervals[i]; } mean /= (float)sqi.intervalCount; float variance = 0.0f; for (uint8_t i = 0; i < sqi.intervalCount; i++) { float diff = sqi.lastIntervals[i] - mean; variance += diff * diff; } sqi.intervalVariance = variance / (float)sqi.intervalCount; } } void updateSQI(uint32_t irValue, uint32_t timestamp) { // --- Contact Score --- if (irValue < PROC::CONTACT_THRESHOLD_MIN) { sqi.contactScore = 0.0f; sqi.contactDetected = false; } else if (irValue > PROC::CONTACT_THRESHOLD_MAX) { sqi.contactScore = 0.2f; // Saturated — poor sqi.contactDetected = false; } else { // Optimal range scoring float normalized = (float)(irValue - PROC::CONTACT_THRESHOLD_MIN) / (float)(PROC::CONTACT_THRESHOLD_MAX - PROC::CONTACT_THRESHOLD_MIN); // Best when normalized is 0.2–0.7 (not too low, not saturating) if (normalized < 0.1f) { sqi.contactScore = 0.3f + 0.4f * (normalized / 0.1f); } else if (normalized < 0.7f) { sqi.contactScore = 1.0f; } else { sqi.contactScore = 1.0f - 0.5f * ((normalized - 0.7f) / 0.3f); } sqi.contactDetected = true; } // --- DC Estimate --- static const float DC_ALPHA = 0.001f; static float dcEst = 0.0f; if (dcEst < 1.0f) { dcEst = (float)irValue; } else { dcEst = dcEst * (1.0f - DC_ALPHA) + (float)irValue * DC_ALPHA; } sqi.dcLevel = (uint32_t)dcEst; // --- AC Estimate --- static float runMax = 0.0f; static float runMin = 1000000.0f; static uint32_t winStart = 0; float fv = (float)irValue; if (fv > runMax) runMax = fv; if (fv < runMin) runMin = fv; if (timestamp - winStart > 2000) { sqi.signalAmplitude = runMax - runMin; runMax = fv; runMin = fv; winStart = timestamp; } // --- Perfusion --- if (dcEst > 0) { sqi.perfusionRatio = sqi.signalAmplitude / dcEst; } else { sqi.perfusionRatio = 0.0f; } sqi.perfusionScore = computeScoreLinear( sqi.perfusionRatio, SQI::PERF_MINIMUM, SQI::PERF_GOOD, SQI::PERF_EXCELLENT); // --- Amplitude Score --- sqi.amplitudeScore = computeScoreLinear( sqi.signalAmplitude, SQI::AMP_MINIMUM, SQI::AMP_GOOD, SQI::AMP_EXCELLENT); // --- Stability Score --- if (sqi.intervalCount >= 3) { sqi.stabilityScore = computeScoreInverse( sqi.intervalVariance, SQI::VAR_EXCELLENT, SQI::VAR_GOOD, SQI::VAR_MAXIMUM); } else { sqi.stabilityScore = 0.3f; // Unknown — moderate penalty } // --- Composite SQI --- sqi.composite = SQI::W_CONTACT * sqi.contactScore + SQI::W_PERFUSION * sqi.perfusionScore + SQI::W_STABILITY * sqi.stabilityScore + SQI::W_AMPLITUDE * sqi.amplitudeScore; // Floor: if no contact, composite is zero regardless of other scores if (!sqi.contactDetected) { sqi.composite = 0.0f; } } void resetSQI() { sqi = {0}; } // ============================================================================ // [4] MANUFACTURING CALIBRATION — NVM STORAGE // ============================================================================ namespace CAL { static const char* const NVM_NAMESPACE = "hrcal"; static const uint32_t MAGIC_NUMBER = 0x48524D31; // "HRM1" static const uint8_t CAL_VERSION = 1; } struct CalibrationData { uint32_t magic; // Validation marker uint8_t version; // Calibration data version // Per-unit LED current offsets (factory trim) int8_t redLedOffset; // Added to computed RED PA int8_t irLedOffset; // Added to computed IR PA // Contact threshold trim int16_t contactThresholdOffset; // BPM offset correction float bpmOffset; // Added to final BPM // Amplitude scaling float amplitudeScale; // Multiplied with amplitude threshold // SQI threshold override float sqiMinOverride; // 0 = use default // Manufacturing info uint32_t serialNumber; uint32_t calDateUnix; // Unix timestamp of calibration uint16_t calTemperature; // Temperature during cal (°C × 10) // Checksum uint8_t checksum; }; CalibrationData calData = {0}; Preferences nvmStorage; uint8_t computeCalChecksum(const CalibrationData& data) { const uint8_t* bytes = (const uint8_t*)&data; uint8_t sum = 0; // Checksum covers everything except the checksum field itself size_t len = sizeof(CalibrationData) - sizeof(uint8_t); for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i++) { sum ^= bytes[i]; sum = (sum << 1) | (sum >> 7); // Rotate left } return sum; } bool loadCalibration() { nvmStorage.begin(CAL::NVM_NAMESPACE, true); // Read-only size_t len = nvmStorage.getBytes("cal", &calData, sizeof(CalibrationData)); nvmStorage.end(); if (len != sizeof(CalibrationData)) { Serial.println(F("[CAL] No calibration data found — using defaults")); setDefaultCalibration(); return false; } if (calData.magic != CAL::MAGIC_NUMBER) { Serial.println(F("[CAL] Invalid magic number — using defaults")); setDefaultCalibration(); return false; } if (calData.version != CAL::CAL_VERSION) { Serial.print(F("[CAL] Version mismatch (stored=")); Serial.print(calData.version); Serial.print(F(", expected=")); Serial.print(CAL::CAL_VERSION); Serial.println(F(") — using defaults")); setDefaultCalibration(); return false; } uint8_t expectedChecksum = computeCalChecksum(calData); if (calData.checksum != expectedChecksum) { Serial.println(F("[CAL] Checksum mismatch — using defaults")); setDefaultCalibration(); return false; } Serial.print(F("[CAL] Calibration loaded. S/N: ")); Serial.print(calData.serialNumber); Serial.print(F(" BPM offset: ")); Serial.print(calData.bpmOffset, 2); Serial.print(F(" LED trim: RED=")); Serial.print(calData.redLedOffset); Serial.print(F(" IR=")); Serial.println(calData.irLedOffset); return true; } bool saveCalibration() { calData.magic = CAL::MAGIC_NUMBER; calData.version = CAL::CAL_VERSION; calData.checksum = computeCalChecksum(calData); nvmStorage.begin(CAL::NVM_NAMESPACE, false); // Read-write size_t written = nvmStorage.putBytes("cal", &calData, sizeof(CalibrationData)); nvmStorage.end(); if (written != sizeof(CalibrationData)) { Serial.println(F("[CAL] ERROR: Failed to write calibration")); return false; } Serial.println(F("[CAL] Calibration saved to NVM")); return true; } void setDefaultCalibration() { calData.magic = CAL::MAGIC_NUMBER; calData.version = CAL::CAL_VERSION; calData.redLedOffset = 0; calData.irLedOffset = 0; calData.contactThresholdOffset = 0; calData.bpmOffset = 0.0f; calData.amplitudeScale = 1.0f; calData.sqiMinOverride = 0.0f; calData.serialNumber = 0; calData.calDateUnix = 0; calData.calTemperature = 250; // 25.0°C calData.checksum = computeCalChecksum(calData); } // Apply calibration offsets to LED current uint8_t applyLedCalibration(uint8_t basePA, int8_t offset) { int16_t adjusted = (int16_t)basePA + (int16_t)offset; return (uint8_t)constrain(adjusted, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MIN, LED_CTRL::LED_PA_MAX); } // Apply calibration offset to BPM float applyBpmCalibration(float rawBpm) { return rawBpm + calData.bpmOffset; } // Get effective contact threshold uint32_t getEffectiveContactThreshold() { int32_t adjusted = (int32_t)PROC::CONTACT_THRESHOLD_MIN + (int32_t)calData.contactThresholdOffset; return (uint32_t)max((int32_t)1000, adjusted); } // Get effective SQI minimum float getEffectiveSqiMin() { if (calData.sqiMinOverride > 0.01f) { return calData.sqiMinOverride; } return REGULATORY::MIN_SQI_FOR_OUTPUT; } // Factory calibration command processor (via Serial) void processCalibrationCommand(const String& cmd); // ============================================================================ // [1] TIMER-DRIVEN DETERMINISTIC ACQUISITION // ============================================================================ // Hardware timer for deterministic timing hw_timer_t* acquisitionTimer = NULL; volatile bool acquisitionFlag = false; volatile uint32_t timerTickCount = 0; volatile uint32_t timerMissCount = 0; // Timing statistics struct TimingStats { uint32_t totalTicks; uint32_t missedDeadlines; // Flag was still set when timer fired uint32_t maxProcessingUs; // Worst-case processing time uint32_t avgProcessingUs; // Average processing time uint32_t lastProcessingUs; uint32_t processingSum; uint32_t processingCount; uint32_t lastTickTimestamp; uint32_t worstJitterUs; // Max deviation from expected interval }; TimingStats timing = {0}; // Timer interval: 10ms (100 Hz check rate) // At 25 effective samples/sec, this ensures we never miss data static const uint32_t TIMER_INTERVAL_US = 10000; // 10ms = 10000µs static const uint32_t EXPECTED_INTERVAL_US = 10000; // Timer ISR — minimal, just sets flag void IRAM_ATTR onAcquisitionTimer() { if (acquisitionFlag) { // Previous cycle not completed — deadline miss timerMissCount++; } acquisitionFlag = true; timerTickCount++; } void setupAcquisitionTimer() { // ESP32-C3 timer: timer 0, prescaler for 1MHz tick (1µs resolution) acquisitionTimer = timerBegin(0, 80, true); // 80MHz / 80 = 1MHz timerAttachInterrupt(acquisitionTimer, &onAcquisitionTimer, true); timerAlarmWrite(acquisitionTimer, TIMER_INTERVAL_US, true); // Auto-reload timerAlarmEnable(acquisitionTimer); Serial.print(F("[TIMER] Acquisition timer started: ")); Serial.print(TIMER_INTERVAL_US); Serial.println(F("µs interval (deterministic)")); } void recordTimingStats(uint32_t processingStartUs) { uint32_t processingUs = micros() - processingStartUs; timing.totalTicks++; timing.lastProcessingUs = processingUs; timing.processingSum += processingUs; timing.processingCount++; timing.avgProcessingUs = timing.processingSum / timing.processingCount; if (processingUs > timing.maxProcessingUs) { timing.maxProcessingUs = processingUs; } // Jitter measurement uint32_t now = micros(); if (timing.lastTickTimestamp > 0) { uint32_t actual = now - timing.lastTickTimestamp; int32_t jitter = (int32_t)actual - (int32_t)EXPECTED_INTERVAL_US; uint32_t absJitter = (uint32_t)abs(jitter); if (absJitter > timing.worstJitterUs) { timing.worstJitterUs = absJitter; } } timing.lastTickTimestamp = now; timing.missedDeadlines = timerMissCount; } // ============================================================================ // [5] VERIFICATION FRAMEWORK // ============================================================================ namespace VERIFY { static const uint8_t MAX_REPLAY_SAMPLES = 200; // Limited by RAM static const uint8_t NUM_NOISE_LEVELS = 5; } struct VerificationStats { // Replay test results uint32_t replayBeatsDetected; uint32_t replayBeatsExpected; float replayAvgBpm; float replayExpectedBpm; float replayError; bool replayPassed; // Noise injection results float noiseLevel; float noiseBpmError; float noiseSqiDrop; bool noiseSuppressed; // Did output get suppressed at high noise? // Statistical summary uint32_t totalTestsRun; uint32_t totalTestsPassed; float meanAbsoluteError; }; VerificationStats verifyStats = {0}; // Generate synthetic PPG signal for testing // Returns a sinusoidal pulse at specified BPM float generateSyntheticPPG(uint32_t sampleIndex, float bpm, float amplitude, float dcOffset, float noiseLevel) { float heartRate = bpm / 60.0f; // Hz float sampleRate = (float)SENSOR_CFG::EFFECTIVE_RATE; float t = (float)sampleIndex / sampleRate; // Primary pulse wave (fundamental) float pulse = amplitude * sin(2.0f * PI * heartRate * t); // Add second harmonic (realistic PPG shape) pulse += amplitude * 0.3f * sin(2.0f * PI * 2.0f * heartRate * t + 0.5f); // Add DC offset pulse += dcOffset; // Add noise if (noiseLevel > 0.0f) { // Simple pseudo-random noise using sample index float noise = noiseLevel * ((float)((sampleIndex * 1103515245 + 12345) % 1000) / 500.0f - 1.0f); pulse += noise; } return pulse; } // Replay test: run known synthetic signal through beat detector void runReplayTest(float targetBpm, float amplitude, float dcOffset, float noise) { Serial.println(F("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━")); Serial.print(F("[VERIFY] Replay test: ")); Serial.print(targetBpm, 0); Serial.print(F(" BPM, amp=")); Serial.print(amplitude, 0); Serial.print(F(", noise=")); Serial.println(noise, 1); // Save current state float savedThreshold = dynamicThreshold; float savedPeak = peakValue; float savedValley = valleyValue; bool savedPhase = isPeakPhase; uint32_t savedLastBeat = lastBeatTime; float savedPrevFiltered = prevFilteredIR; bool savedRefractory = inRefractory; // Reset detector for test dynamicThreshold = 0.0f; peakValue = 0.0f; valleyValue = 0.0f; isPeakPhase = false; lastBeatTime = 0; prevFilteredIR = 0.0f; inRefractory = false; refractoryEnd = 0; bpFilter.initialized = false; uint32_t beatsDetected = 0; float bpmSum = 0.0f; uint8_t bpmCount = 0; // Run 8 seconds of synthetic data uint32_t totalSamples = 8 * SENSOR_CFG::EFFECTIVE_RATE; uint32_t msPerSample = 1000 / SENSOR_CFG::EFFECTIVE_RATE; for (uint32_t i = 0; i < totalSamples; i++) { float sample = generateSyntheticPPG(i, targetBpm, amplitude, dcOffset, noise); float filtered = applyBandpassFilter(sample); uint32_t fakeTimestamp = i * msPerSample; BeatResult beat = detectBeat(filtered, fakeTimestamp); if (beat.beatDetected) { beatsDetected++; if (beat.instantBpm >= PROC::BPM_MIN && beat.instantBpm <= PROC::BPM_MAX) { bpmSum += beat.instantBpm; bpmCount++; } } } // Expected beats in 8 seconds float expectedBeats = targetBpm / 60.0f * 8.0f; float avgBpm = (bpmCount > 0) ? (bpmSum / (float)bpmCount) : 0.0f; float error = fabsf(avgBpm - targetBpm); verifyStats.replayBeatsDetected = beatsDetected; verifyStats.replayBeatsExpected = (uint32_t)(expectedBeats + 0.5f); verifyStats.replayAvgBpm = avgBpm; verifyStats.replayExpectedBpm = targetBpm; verifyStats.replayError = error; verifyStats.replayPassed = (error <= REGULATORY::CLAIMED_ACCURACY_BPM) && (bpmCount >= 3); Serial.print(F(" Beats detected: ")); Serial.print(beatsDetected); Serial.print(F(" (expected ~")); Serial.print(expectedBeats, 1); Serial.println(F(")")); Serial.print(F(" Avg BPM: ")); Serial.print(avgBpm, 1); Serial.print(F(" (target: ")); Serial.print(targetBpm, 1); Serial.print(F(") Error: ")); Serial.print(error, 1); Serial.println(F(" BPM")); Serial.print(F(" Result: ")); Serial.println(verifyStats.replayPassed ? "PASS" : "FAIL"); verifyStats.totalTestsRun++; if (verifyStats.replayPassed) verifyStats.totalTestsPassed++; // Restore state dynamicThreshold = savedThreshold; peakValue = savedPeak; valleyValue = savedValley; isPeakPhase = savedPhase; lastBeatTime = savedLastBeat; prevFilteredIR = savedPrevFiltered; inRefractory = savedRefractory; bpFilter.initialized = false; // Will re-init on next real sample } // Noise injection test: verify SQI drops and output suppresses appropriately void runNoiseInjectionTest(float baseBpm) { Serial.println(F("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━")); Serial.println(F("[VERIFY] Noise injection test")); float noiseLevels[] = {0.0f, 50.0f, 200.0f, 500.0f, 1000.0f}; float amplitude = 300.0f; float dcOffset = 100000.0f; for (uint8_t n = 0; n < VERIFY::NUM_NOISE_LEVELS; n++) { float noise = noiseLevels[n]; // Reset filter bpFilter.initialized = false; dynamicThreshold = 0.0f; peakValue = 0.0f; valleyValue = 0.0f; isPeakPhase = false; lastBeatTime = 0; prevFilteredIR = 0.0f; inRefractory = false; uint32_t beats = 0; float bpmSum = 0.0f; uint8_t bpmCount = 0; uint32_t totalSamples = 6 * SENSOR_CFG::EFFECTIVE_RATE; uint32_t msPerSample = 1000 / SENSOR_CFG::EFFECTIVE_RATE; for (uint32_t i = 0; i < totalSamples; i++) { float sample = generateSyntheticPPG(i, baseBpm, amplitude, dcOffset, noise); float filtered = applyBandpassFilter(sample); BeatResult beat = detectBeat(filtered, i * msPerSample); if (beat.beatDetected && beat.instantBpm >= PROC::BPM_MIN && beat.instantBpm <= PROC::BPM_MAX) { beats++; bpmSum += beat.instantBpm; bpmCount++; } } float avgBpm = (bpmCount > 0) ? (bpmSum / (float)bpmCount) : 0.0f; float error = fabsf(avgBpm - baseBpm); Serial.print(F(" Noise=")); Serial.print(noise, 0); Serial.print(F(" Beats=")); Serial.print(beats); Serial.print(F(" BPM=")); Serial.print(avgBpm, 1); Serial.print(F(" Error=")); Serial.print(error, 1); Serial.print(F(" ")); Serial.println((error <= REGULATORY::CLAIMED_ACCURACY_BPM || bpmCount == 0) ? "OK" : "DEGRADED"); } bpFilter.initialized = false; // Reset for real operation } // Full verification suite void runVerificationSuite() { Serial.println(); Serial.println(F("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════╗")); Serial.println(F("║ VERIFICATION SUITE — SELF-TEST ║")); Serial.println(F("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════╝")); Serial.println(); verifyStats.totalTestsRun = 0; verifyStats.totalTestsPassed = 0; // Test 1: Known heart rates Serial.println(F("[TEST 1] Replay at known heart rates")); runReplayTest(60.0f, 300.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // 60 BPM, clean runReplayTest(72.0f, 300.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // 72 BPM, clean runReplayTest(100.0f, 300.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // 100 BPM, clean runReplayTest(120.0f, 300.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // 120 BPM, clean runReplayTest(150.0f, 300.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // 150 BPM, clean // Test 2: Boundary conditions Serial.println(); Serial.println(F("[TEST 2] Boundary BPM values")); runReplayTest(42.0f, 300.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // Near minimum runReplayTest(175.0f, 300.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // Near maximum // Test 3: Low amplitude (poor perfusion simulation) Serial.println(); Serial.println(F("[TEST 3] Low amplitude signals")); runReplayTest(72.0f, 80.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // Low amplitude runReplayTest(72.0f, 40.0f, 100000.0f, 0.0f); // Very low amplitude // Test 4: Noise injection Serial.println(); Serial.println(F("[TEST 4] Noise injection")); runNoiseInjectionTest(72.0f); // Summary Serial.println(); Serial.println(F("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━")); Serial.print(F("[VERIFY] TOTAL: ")); Serial.print(verifyStats.totalTestsPassed); Serial.print(F(" / ")); Serial.print(verifyStats.totalTestsRun); Serial.println(F(" tests passed")); Serial.println(F("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━")); Serial.println(); } // ============================================================================ // DATA TYPES // ============================================================================ enum class State : uint8_t { INIT, NO_CONTACT, ACQUIRING, TRACKING, UNSTABLE, ERROR_STATE }; const char* stateName(State s) { switch (s) { case State::INIT: return "INIT"; case State::NO_CONTACT: return "NO_CONTACT"; case State::ACQUIRING: return "ACQUIRING"; case State::TRACKING: return "TRACKING"; case State::UNSTABLE: return "UNSTABLE"; case State::ERROR_STATE: return "ERROR"; default: return "UNKNOWN"; } } struct BeatResult { bool beatDetected; uint32_t beatTimestamp; uint16_t intervalMs; float instantBpm; float amplitude; }; struct HysteresisState { float referenceValue; float candidateValue; uint32_t candidateStartMs; bool isLocked; bool candidateActive; float outputValue; bool outputValid; }; // ============================================================================ // GLOBAL STATE // ============================================================================ State currentState = State::INIT; State previousState = State::INIT; uint32_t stateEntryTime = 0; // Sensor data buffers uint32_t irBuffer[32]; uint32_t redBuffer[32]; // Signal processing float filteredIR = 0.0f; float prevFilteredIR = 0.0f; // Beat detection (PBA) float dynamicThreshold = 0.0f; float peakValue = 0.0f; float valleyValue = 0.0f; bool isPeakPhase = false; uint32_t lastBeatTime = 0; uint8_t consecutiveMissed = 0; bool inRefractory = false; uint32_t refractoryEnd = 0; // BPM averaging float bpmBuffer[PROC::ROLLING_AVG_SIZE]; uint8_t bpmBufferIndex = 0; uint8_t bpmBufferCount = 0; float rollingAvgBpm = 0.0f; float emaBpm = 0.0f; bool emaInitialized = false; // Beat counting uint32_t totalBeatsDetected = 0; uint32_t acquiringStartTime = 0; // Hysteresis HysteresisState hysteresis = {0}; // Error tracking uint8_t i2cErrorCount = 0; uint32_t lastSuccessfulRead = 0; // Output timing uint32_t lastOutputTime = 0; static const uint32_t OUTPUT_INTERVAL_MS = 500; // [6] Continuous use tracking uint32_t sessionStartTime = 0; bool continuousUseWarned = false; // Status LED bool statusLedState = false; uint32_t lastLedToggle = 0; // [5] Timing diagnostics output uint32_t lastDiagTime = 0; static const uint32_t DIAG_INTERVAL_MS = 10000; // Every 10 seconds // ============================================================================ // DIGITAL FILTERING // ============================================================================ struct BandpassFilter { float hpPrev_x; float hpPrev_y; float hpAlpha; float lpPrev_y; float lpAlpha; bool initialized; }; BandpassFilter bpFilter = {0, 0, 0.95f, 0, 0.15f, false}; float applyBandpassFilter(float input) { if (!bpFilter.initialized) { bpFilter.hpPrev_x = input; bpFilter.hpPrev_y = 0.0f; bpFilter.lpPrev_y = 0.0f; bpFilter.initialized = true; return 0.0f; } float hpOut = bpFilter.hpAlpha * (bpFilter.hpPrev_y + input - bpFilter.hpPrev_x); bpFilter.hpPrev_x = input; bpFilter.hpPrev_y = hpOut; float lpOut = bpFilter.lpAlpha * hpOut + (1.0f - bpFilter.lpAlpha) * bpFilter.lpPrev_y; bpFilter.lpPrev_y = lpOut; return lpOut; } // ============================================================================ // I²C COMMUNICATION // ============================================================================ bool writeRegister(uint8_t reg, uint8_t value) { Wire.beginTransmission(MAX30105_ADDR); Wire.write(reg); Wire.write(value); uint8_t error = Wire.endTransmission(); if (error != 0) { i2cErrorCount++; return false; } i2cErrorCount = 0; return true; } uint8_t readRegister(uint8_t reg) { Wire.beginTransmission(MAX30105_ADDR); Wire.write(reg); uint8_t error = Wire.endTransmission(false); if (error != 0) { i2cErrorCount++; return 0; } Wire.requestFrom(MAX30105_ADDR, (uint8_t)1); if (Wire.available()) { i2cErrorCount = 0; return Wire.read(); } i2cErrorCount++; return 0; } bool readMultipleBytes(uint8_t reg, uint8_t* buffer, uint8_t length) { Wire.beginTransmission(MAX30105_ADDR); Wire.write(reg); uint8_t error = Wire.endTransmission(false); if (error != 0) { i2cErrorCount++; return false; } Wire.requestFrom(MAX30105_ADDR, length); uint8_t received = 0; while (Wire.available() && received < length) { buffer[received++] = Wire.read(); } if (received != length) { i2cErrorCount++; return false; } i2cErrorCount = 0; return true; } // ============================================================================ // MAX30105 SENSOR SETUP // ============================================================================ bool detectSensor() { Wire.beginTransmission(MAX30105_ADDR); uint8_t error = Wire.endTransmission(); if (error != 0) { Serial.println(F("[INIT] MAX30105 not found on I2C bus")); return false; } uint8_t partId = readRegister(REG::PART_ID); if (partId != 0x15) { Serial.print(F("[INIT] Unexpected part ID: 0x")); Serial.println(partId, HEX); return false; } uint8_t revId = readRegister(REG::REV_ID); Serial.print(F("[INIT] MAX30105 detected. Part: 0x")); Serial.print(partId, HEX); Serial.print(F(" Rev: 0x")); Serial.println(revId, HEX); return true; } bool resetSensor() { if (!writeRegister(REG::MODE_CONFIG, 0x40)) return false; uint32_t timeout = millis() + 100; while (millis() < timeout) { uint8_t mode = readRegister(REG::MODE_CONFIG); if ((mode & 0x40) == 0) { Serial.println(F("[INIT] Sensor reset complete")); return true; } delay(1); } Serial.println(F("[INIT] Sensor reset timeout")); return false; } bool configureSensor() { Serial.println(F("[CONFIG] Configuring MAX30105...")); if (!writeRegister(REG::FIFO_WRITE_PTR, 0x00)) return false; if (!writeRegister(REG::FIFO_OVERFLOW, 0x00)) return false; if (!writeRegister(REG::FIFO_READ_PTR, 0x00)) return false; uint8_t fifoConfig = (SENSOR_CFG::FIFO_AVG << 5); if (SENSOR_CFG::FIFO_ROLLOVER) fifoConfig |= 0x10; fifoConfig |= 0x0F; if (!writeRegister(REG::FIFO_CONFIG, fifoConfig)) return false; if (!writeRegister(REG::MODE_CONFIG, SENSOR_CFG::MODE)) return false; uint8_t spo2Config = (SENSOR_CFG::ADC_RANGE << 5) | (SENSOR_CFG::SAMPLE_RATE << 2) | (SENSOR_CFG::PULSE_WIDTH); if (!writeRegister(REG::SPO2_CONFIG, spo2Config)) return false; // Apply calibration offsets to initial LED currents uint8_t redPA = applyLedCalibration(SENSOR_CFG::INITIAL_RED_PA, calData.redLedOffset); uint8_t irPA = applyLedCalibration(SENSOR_CFG::INITIAL_IR_PA, calData.irLedOffset); if (!writeRegister(REG::LED1_PA, redPA)) return false; if (!writeRegister(REG::LED2_PA, irPA)) return false; ledCtrl.currentRedPA = redPA; ledCtrl.currentIrPA = irPA; if (!writeRegister(REG::INT_ENABLE_1, 0xC0)) return false; if (!writeRegister(REG::INT_ENABLE_2, 0x00)) return false; readRegister(REG::INT_STATUS_1); readRegister(REG::INT_STATUS_2); Serial.println(F("[CONFIG] Configuration complete")); Serial.print(F(" Mode: SpO2, Rate: 100Hz/4=25Hz, ADC: 16384, PW: 411us")); Serial.println(); Serial.print(F(" LED: RED=")); Serial.print(redPA * 0.2f, 1); Serial.print(F("mA (cal=")); Serial.print(calData.redLedOffset); Serial.print(F("), IR=")); Serial.print(irPA * 0.2f, 1); Serial.print(F("mA (cal=")); Serial.print(calData.irLedOffset); Serial.println(F(")")); return true; } // ============================================================================ // FIFO DATA ACQUISITION // ============================================================================ uint8_t readFIFO(uint32_t* irData, uint32_t* redData, uint8_t maxSamples) { uint8_t writePtr = readRegister(REG::FIFO_WRITE_PTR); uint8_t readPtr = readRegister(REG::FIFO_READ_PTR); uint8_t overflow = readRegister(REG::FIFO_OVERFLOW); if (overflow > 0) { writeRegister(REG::FIFO_WRITE_PTR, 0x00); writeRegister(REG::FIFO_OVERFLOW, 0x00); writeRegister(REG::FIFO_READ_PTR, 0x00); return 0; } int16_t numSamples = (int16_t)writePtr - (int16_t)readPtr; if (numSamples < 0) numSamples += 32; if (numSamples == 0) return 0; if (numSamples > maxSamples) numSamples = maxSamples; uint8_t samplesRead = 0; for (uint8_t i = 0; i < numSamples; i++) { uint8_t fifoBytes[6]; if (!readMultipleBytes(REG::FIFO_DATA, fifoBytes, 6)) break; redData[i] = ((uint32_t)(fifoBytes[0] & 0x03) << 16) | ((uint32_t)fifoBytes[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)fifoBytes[2]); irData[i] = ((uint32_t)(fifoBytes[3] & 0x03) << 16) | ((uint32_t)fifoBytes[4] << 8) | ((uint32_t)fifoBytes[5]); samplesRead++; } lastSuccessfulRead = millis(); return samplesRead; } // ============================================================================ // BEAT DETECTION — PBA // ============================================================================ BeatResult detectBeat(float sample, uint32_t timestamp) { BeatResult result = {false, 0, 0, 0.0f, 0.0f}; if (sample > peakValue) peakValue = sample; if (sample < valleyValue) valleyValue = sample; if (inRefractory) { if (timestamp >= refractoryEnd) { inRefractory = false; } else { dynamicThreshold *= PROC::THRESHOLD_DECAY; prevFilteredIR = sample; return result; } } bool crossingDown = (prevFilteredIR >= dynamicThreshold) && (sample < dynamicThreshold); if (crossingDown && isPeakPhase) { float amplitude = peakValue - valleyValue; // Apply calibration scaling to amplitude threshold float effectiveMinAmplitude = PROC::MIN_BEAT_AMPLITUDE * calData.amplitudeScale; if (amplitude >= effectiveMinAmplitude) { uint32_t interval = timestamp - lastBeatTime; if (interval >= PROC::MIN_BEAT_INTERVAL_MS && interval <= PROC::MAX_BEAT_INTERVAL_MS) { result.beatDetected = true; result.beatTimestamp = timestamp; result.intervalMs = (uint16_t)interval; result.instantBpm = 60000.0f / (float)interval; result.amplitude = amplitude; lastBeatTime = timestamp; consecutiveMissed = 0; totalBeatsDetected++; // [3] Feed interval to SQI addBeatInterval((float)interval); } else if (interval > PROC::MAX_BEAT_INTERVAL_MS) { lastBeatTime = timestamp; consecutiveMissed++; } } dynamicThreshold = peakValue * PROC::THRESHOLD_FACTOR; isPeakPhase = false; valleyValue = sample; inRefractory = true; refractoryEnd = timestamp + PROC::REFRACTORY_MS; } else if (sample > dynamicThreshold && !isPeakPhase) { isPeakPhase = true; peakValue = sample; valleyValue = prevFilteredIR; } dynamicThreshold *= PROC::THRESHOLD_DECAY; float effectiveMinAmp = PROC::MIN_BEAT_AMPLITUDE * calData.amplitudeScale; if (dynamicThreshold < effectiveMinAmp * 0.3f) { dynamicThreshold = effectiveMinAmp * 0.3f; } if (lastBeatTime > 0 && (timestamp - lastBeatTime) > PROC::SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS) { consecutiveMissed++; lastBeatTime = timestamp; } prevFilteredIR = sample; return result; } // ============================================================================ // BPM COMPUTATION AND AVERAGING // ============================================================================ void addBpmToBuffer(float bpm) { if (bpm < PROC::BPM_MIN || bpm > PROC::BPM_MAX) return; bpmBuffer[bpmBufferIndex] = bpm; bpmBufferIndex = (bpmBufferIndex + 1) % PROC::ROLLING_AVG_SIZE; if (bpmBufferCount < PROC::ROLLING_AVG_SIZE) bpmBufferCount++; if (bpmBufferCount > 0) { float sum = 0.0f; for (uint8_t i = 0; i < bpmBufferCount; i++) sum += bpmBuffer[i]; rollingAvgBpm = sum / (float)bpmBufferCount; } if (!emaInitialized) { emaBpm = rollingAvgBpm; emaInitialized = true; } else { emaBpm = PROC::EMA_ALPHA * rollingAvgBpm + (1.0f - PROC::EMA_ALPHA) * emaBpm; } } float getSmoothedBpm() { // [4] Apply calibration BPM offset return applyBpmCalibration(emaBpm); } // ============================================================================ // 6-SECOND TEMPORAL HYSTERESIS // ============================================================================ void updateHysteresis(float currentBpm, uint32_t timestamp) { if (bpmBufferCount < 3) { hysteresis.outputValid = false; return; } float smoothed = getSmoothedBpm(); // [6] Regulatory: reject physiologically impossible values if (smoothed < REGULATORY::PHYSIOLOGIC_BPM_MIN || smoothed > REGULATORY::PHYSIOLOGIC_BPM_MAX) { hysteresis.outputValid = false; return; } if (!hysteresis.isLocked) { if (!hysteresis.candidateActive) { hysteresis.candidateValue = smoothed; hysteresis.candidateStartMs = timestamp; hysteresis.candidateActive = true; return; } float diff = fabsf(smoothed - hysteresis.candidateValue); if (diff <= PROC::HYSTERESIS_BAND) { hysteresis.candidateValue = 0.9f * hysteresis.candidateValue + 0.1f * smoothed; uint32_t elapsed = timestamp - hysteresis.candidateStartMs; if (elapsed >= PROC::HYSTERESIS_INIT_MS) { hysteresis.referenceValue = hysteresis.candidateValue; hysteresis.isLocked = true; hysteresis.outputValue = hysteresis.referenceValue; hysteresis.outputValid = true; hysteresis.candidateActive = false; Serial.print(F("[HYST] Initial lock: ")); Serial.print(hysteresis.referenceValue, 1); Serial.println(F(" BPM")); } } else { hysteresis.candidateValue = smoothed; hysteresis.candidateStartMs = timestamp; } return; } // Locked — check band float diffFromRef = fabsf(smoothed - hysteresis.referenceValue); if (diffFromRef <= PROC::HYSTERESIS_BAND) { hysteresis.outputValue = hysteresis.referenceValue; hysteresis.candidateActive = false; return; } // Outside band — immediate reset hysteresis.isLocked = false; if (!hysteresis.candidateActive) { hysteresis.candidateValue = smoothed; hysteresis.candidateStartMs = timestamp; hysteresis.candidateActive = true; Serial.print(F("[HYST] Lock broken → candidate: ")); Serial.print(smoothed, 1); Serial.println(F(" BPM")); } else { float diffFromCandidate = fabsf(smoothed - hysteresis.candidateValue); if (diffFromCandidate <= PROC::HYSTERESIS_BAND) { hysteresis.candidateValue = 0.9f * hysteresis.candidateValue + 0.1f * smoothed; uint32_t elapsed = timestamp - hysteresis.candidateStartMs; if (elapsed >= PROC::HYSTERESIS_LOCK_MS) { hysteresis.referenceValue = hysteresis.candidateValue; hysteresis.isLocked = true; hysteresis.outputValue = hysteresis.referenceValue; hysteresis.outputValid = true; hysteresis.candidateActive = false; Serial.print(F("[HYST] New lock: ")); Serial.print(hysteresis.referenceValue, 1); Serial.println(F(" BPM")); } } else { hysteresis.candidateValue = smoothed; hysteresis.candidateStartMs = timestamp; } } } void resetHysteresis() { hysteresis = {0, 0, 0, false, false, 0, false}; } // ============================================================================ // STATE MACHINE // ============================================================================ void resetProcessingState() { filteredIR = 0.0f; prevFilteredIR = 0.0f; bpFilter.initialized = false; dynamicThreshold = 0.0f; peakValue = 0.0f; valleyValue = 0.0f; isPeakPhase = false; lastBeatTime = 0; consecutiveMissed = 0; inRefractory = false; refractoryEnd = 0; bpmBufferIndex = 0; bpmBufferCount = 0; rollingAvgBpm = 0.0f; emaBpm = 0.0f; emaInitialized = false; totalBeatsDetected = 0; resetHysteresis(); resetSQI(); continuousUseWarned = false; } void transitionTo(State newState) { if (newState == currentState) return; Serial.print(F("[STATE] ")); Serial.print(stateName(currentState)); Serial.print(F(" → ")); Serial.println(stateName(newState)); previousState = currentState; currentState = newState; stateEntryTime = millis(); switch (newState) { case State::INIT: break; case State::NO_CONTACT: resetProcessingState(); break; case State::ACQUIRING: acquiringStartTime = millis(); totalBeatsDetected = 0; resetHysteresis(); sessionStartTime = millis(); continuousUseWarned = false; break; case State::TRACKING: Serial.println(F("[STATE] Tracking — output valid")); break; case State::UNSTABLE: break; case State::ERROR_STATE: Serial.println(F("[STATE] ERROR — recovery pending")); break; } } void updateStateMachine() { uint32_t now = millis(); uint32_t timeInState = now - stateEntryTime; switch (currentState) { case State::INIT: if (sqi.contactDetected) { transitionTo(State::ACQUIRING); } else { transitionTo(State::NO_CONTACT); } break; case State::NO_CONTACT: if (i2cErrorCount > 10) { transitionTo(State::ERROR_STATE); return; } if (sqi.contactDetected) { transitionTo(State::ACQUIRING); } break; case State::ACQUIRING: if (!sqi.contactDetected) { transitionTo(State::NO_CONTACT); return; } if (i2cErrorCount > 10) { transitionTo(State::ERROR_STATE); return; } if (timeInState >= PROC::ACQUIRING_MIN_MS && totalBeatsDetected >= PROC::MIN_BEATS_FOR_TRACKING && bpmBufferCount >= 3 && hysteresis.outputValid && sqi.composite >= getEffectiveSqiMin()) { // [3] SQI gate transitionTo(State::TRACKING); } break; case State::TRACKING: if (!sqi.contactDetected) { transitionTo(State::NO_CONTACT); return; } if (consecutiveMissed >= PROC::MAX_MISSED_BEATS) { transitionTo(State::UNSTABLE); return; } // [3] SQI drop below threshold if (sqi.composite < getEffectiveSqiMin() * 0.8f) { transitionTo(State::UNSTABLE); return; } if (i2cErrorCount > 10) { transitionTo(State::ERROR_STATE); return; } break; case State::UNSTABLE: if (!sqi.contactDetected) { transitionTo(State::NO_CONTACT); return; } // Recovery → ACQUIRING (not direct to TRACKING) if (consecutiveMissed == 0 && bpmBufferCount >= 3 && sqi.composite >= getEffectiveSqiMin()) { transitionTo(State::ACQUIRING); return; } if (timeInState > 10000) { transitionTo(State::NO_CONTACT); return; } if (i2cErrorCount > 10) { transitionTo(State::ERROR_STATE); return; } break; case State::ERROR_STATE: if (timeInState > 2000) { Serial.println(F("[ERROR] Attempting recovery...")); i2cErrorCount = 0; transitionTo(State::INIT); } break; } } // ============================================================================ // STATUS LED // ============================================================================ void updateStatusLed() { uint32_t now = millis(); uint32_t interval; switch (currentState) { case State::INIT: interval = 100; break; case State::NO_CONTACT: interval = 1000; break; case State::ACQUIRING: interval = 500; break; case State::TRACKING: digitalWrite(PIN_STATUS_LED, LOW); return; case State::UNSTABLE: interval = 200; break; case State::ERROR_STATE: interval = 100; break; default: interval = 1000; break; } if (interval > 0 && (now - lastLedToggle >= interval)) { statusLedState = !statusLedState; digitalWrite(PIN_STATUS_LED, statusLedState ? LOW : HIGH); lastLedToggle = now; } } // ============================================================================ // [6] GUARDED OUTPUT — REGULATORY-COMPLIANT // ============================================================================ void outputResults() { uint32_t now = millis(); if (now - lastOutputTime < OUTPUT_INTERVAL_MS) return; lastOutputTime = now; // [6] Continuous use warning if (currentState == State::TRACKING && !continuousUseWarned) { if (now - sessionStartTime > REGULATORY::MAX_CONTINUOUS_USE_MS) { Serial.println(F("[WARN] Extended continuous use. Take a break.")); continuousUseWarned = true; } } Serial.print(F("[")); Serial.print(stateName(currentState)); Serial.print(F("]")); // [3] Print SQI Serial.print(F(" SQI:")); Serial.print(sqi.composite, 2); switch (currentState) { case State::NO_CONTACT: Serial.println(F(" Place finger on sensor")); break; case State::ACQUIRING: Serial.print(F(" Acquiring... Beats:")); Serial.print(totalBeatsDetected); if (bpmBufferCount > 0) { Serial.print(F(" Raw:")); Serial.print(rollingAvgBpm, 1); } Serial.print(F(" IR:")); Serial.print(sqi.dcLevel); Serial.print(F(" LED:")); Serial.print(ledCtrl.currentIrPA * 0.2f, 1); Serial.println(F("mA")); break; case State::TRACKING: { // [6] Suppress output if SQI too low if (sqi.composite < REGULATORY::MIN_SQI_FOR_DISPLAY) { Serial.println(F(" BPM: --- (signal quality too low)")); break; } // [6] Suppress if data too old uint32_t dataAge = now - lastSuccessfulRead; if (dataAge > REGULATORY::MAX_OUTPUT_AGE_MS) { Serial.println(F(" BPM: --- (stale data)")); break; } // [6] Output with regulatory prefix Serial.print(F(" BPM: ")); Serial.print(REGULATORY::OUTPUT_PREFIX); if (hysteresis.outputValid && sqi.composite >= REGULATORY::MIN_SQI_FOR_OUTPUT) { Serial.print((int)hysteresis.outputValue); } else if (sqi.composite >= REGULATORY::MIN_SQI_FOR_DISPLAY) { Serial.print((int)getSmoothedBpm()); Serial.print(F("?")); // Uncertain } else { Serial.print(F("---")); } Serial.print(F(" Lock:")); Serial.print(hysteresis.isLocked ? "Y" : "N"); if (hysteresis.isLocked) { Serial.print(F(" Ref:")); Serial.print(hysteresis.referenceValue, 1); } Serial.print(F(" LED:")); Serial.print(ledCtrl.currentIrPA * 0.2f, 1); Serial.println(F("mA")); break; } case State::UNSTABLE: Serial.print(F(" Signal unstable. Missed:")); Serial.print(consecutiveMissed); if (hysteresis.outputValue > 0) { Serial.print(F(" Last:")); Serial.print(REGULATORY::OUTPUT_PREFIX); Serial.print((int)hysteresis.outputValue); } Serial.println(); break; case State::ERROR_STATE: Serial.print(F(" I2C errors:")); Serial.println(i2cErrorCount); break; default: Serial.println(); break; } } // [1] Timing diagnostics output void outputTimingDiagnostics() { uint32_t now = millis(); if (now - lastDiagTime < DIAG_INTERVAL_MS) return; lastDiagTime = now; Serial.println(F("┌─ TIMING DIAGNOSTICS ─────────────────────┐")); Serial.print(F("│ Ticks: ")); Serial.print(timing.totalTicks); Serial.print(F(" Missed: ")); Serial.print(timing.missedDeadlines); Serial.print(F(" (")); if (timing.totalTicks > 0) { Serial.print(100.0f * (float)timing.missedDeadlines / (float)timing.totalTicks, 2); } else { Serial.print(F("0.00")); } Serial.println(F("%)")); Serial.print(F("│ Processing: avg=")); Serial.print(timing.avgProcessingUs); Serial.print(F("µs max=")); Serial.print(timing.maxProcessingUs); Serial.println(F("µs")); Serial.print(F("│ Jitter: worst=")); Serial.print(timing.worstJitterUs); Serial.println(F("µs")); Serial.print(F("│ LED adjustments: ")); Serial.println(ledCtrl.adjustmentCount); if (calData.serialNumber > 0) { Serial.print(F("│ Cal S/N: ")); Serial.println(calData.serialNumber); } Serial.println(F("└──────────────────────────────────────────┘")); } // ============================================================================ // [4] FACTORY CALIBRATION COMMAND INTERFACE // ============================================================================ void processCalibrationCommand(const String& cmd) { // Commands: // CAL:READ - Read current calibration // CAL:DEFAULT - Reset to defaults // CAL:SN:12345 - Set serial number // CAL:BPM:+1.5 - Set BPM offset // CAL:RED:-2 - Set RED LED offset // CAL:IR:+3 - Set IR LED offset // CAL:CONTACT:500 - Set contact threshold offset // CAL:AMP:1.1 - Set amplitude scale // CAL:SQIMIN:0.45 - Set SQI minimum override // CAL:SAVE - Write to NVM // CAL:VERIFY - Run verification suite // CAL:TIMING - Print timing stats if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:READ")) { Serial.println(F("┌─ CALIBRATION DATA ───────────────────────┐")); Serial.print(F("│ S/N: ")); Serial.println(calData.serialNumber); Serial.print(F("│ BPM offset: ")); Serial.println(calData.bpmOffset, 3); Serial.print(F("│ RED LED offset: ")); Serial.println(calData.redLedOffset); Serial.print(F("│ IR LED offset: ")); Serial.println(calData.irLedOffset); Serial.print(F("│ Contact offset: ")); Serial.println(calData.contactThresholdOffset); Serial.print(F("│ Amplitude scale: ")); Serial.println(calData.amplitudeScale, 3); Serial.print(F("│ SQI min override: ")); Serial.println(calData.sqiMinOverride, 3); Serial.print(F("│ Cal temp: ")); Serial.print(calData.calTemperature / 10.0f, 1); Serial.println(F("°C")); Serial.println(F("└──────────────────────────────────────────┘")); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:DEFAULT")) { setDefaultCalibration(); Serial.println(F("[CAL] Reset to defaults")); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:SN:")) { calData.serialNumber = cmd.substring(7).toInt(); Serial.print(F("[CAL] S/N = ")); Serial.println(calData.serialNumber); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:BPM:")) { calData.bpmOffset = cmd.substring(8).toFloat(); Serial.print(F("[CAL] BPM offset = ")); Serial.println(calData.bpmOffset, 3); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:RED:")) { calData.redLedOffset = (int8_t)cmd.substring(8).toInt(); Serial.print(F("[CAL] RED offset = ")); Serial.println(calData.redLedOffset); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:IR:")) { calData.irLedOffset = (int8_t)cmd.substring(7).toInt(); Serial.print(F("[CAL] IR offset = ")); Serial.println(calData.irLedOffset); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:CONTACT:")) { calData.contactThresholdOffset = (int16_t)cmd.substring(12).toInt(); Serial.print(F("[CAL] Contact offset = ")); Serial.println(calData.contactThresholdOffset); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:AMP:")) { calData.amplitudeScale = cmd.substring(8).toFloat(); if (calData.amplitudeScale < 0.1f) calData.amplitudeScale = 0.1f; if (calData.amplitudeScale > 10.0f) calData.amplitudeScale = 10.0f; Serial.print(F("[CAL] Amplitude scale = ")); Serial.println(calData.amplitudeScale, 3); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:SQIMIN:")) { calData.sqiMinOverride = cmd.substring(11).toFloat(); Serial.print(F("[CAL] SQI min = ")); Serial.println(calData.sqiMinOverride, 3); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:SAVE")) { calData.calDateUnix = millis() / 1000; // Approximate saveCalibration(); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:VERIFY")) { runVerificationSuite(); } else if (cmd.startsWith("CAL:TIMING")) { outputTimingDiagnostics(); } else { Serial.println(F("[CAL] Unknown command. Available:")); Serial.println(F(" CAL:READ / CAL:DEFAULT / CAL:SAVE")); Serial.println(F(" CAL:SN:# / CAL:BPM:±# / CAL:RED:±# / CAL:IR:±#")); Serial.println(F(" CAL:CONTACT:# / CAL:AMP:# / CAL:SQIMIN:#")); Serial.println(F(" CAL:VERIFY / CAL:TIMING")); } } // ============================================================================ // MAIN PROCESSING — SINGLE SAMPLE // ============================================================================ void processSample(uint32_t irValue, uint32_t redValue, uint32_t timestamp) { // 1. Signal Quality Index updateSQI(irValue, timestamp); // 2. Adaptive LED Control updateAdaptiveLedControl(sqi.dcLevel, timestamp); // 3. Digital Filtering float filtered = applyBandpassFilter((float)irValue); filteredIR = filtered; // 4. Beat Detection (only if signal valid) if (sqi.contactDetected && sqi.composite >= REGULATORY::MIN_SQI_FOR_DISPLAY) { BeatResult beat = detectBeat(filtered, timestamp); // 5. BPM Computation if (beat.beatDetected) { if (beat.instantBpm >= PROC::BPM_MIN && beat.instantBpm <= PROC::BPM_MAX) { // 6. Averaging addBpmToBuffer(beat.instantBpm); // 7. Hysteresis updateHysteresis(beat.instantBpm, timestamp); } } } // 8. State Machine updateStateMachine(); } // ============================================================================ // SERIAL COMMAND HANDLER // ============================================================================ String serialBuffer = ""; void checkSerialCommands() { while (Serial.available()) { char c = Serial.read(); if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') { if (serialBuffer.length() > 0) { serialBuffer.trim(); serialBuffer.toUpperCase(); if (serialBuffer.startsWith("CAL:")) { processCalibrationCommand(serialBuffer); } else if (serialBuffer == "STATUS") { outputTimingDiagnostics(); Serial.print(F("State: ")); Serial.println(stateName(currentState)); Serial.print(F("SQI: ")); Serial.println(sqi.composite, 3); Serial.print(F("BPM: ")); Serial.println(getSmoothedBpm(), 1); } else if (serialBuffer == "VERIFY") { runVerificationSuite(); } else if (serialBuffer == "HELP") { Serial.println(F("Commands: STATUS / VERIFY / CAL:READ / CAL:HELP / HELP")); } else { Serial.print(F("Unknown: ")); Serial.println(serialBuffer); } serialBuffer = ""; } } else { if (serialBuffer.length() < 64) { serialBuffer += c; } } } } // ============================================================================ // SETUP // ============================================================================ void setup() { Serial.begin(115200); delay(1000); // [6] Regulatory banner — always first printRegulatoryBanner(); // Status LED pinMode(PIN_STATUS_LED, OUTPUT); digitalWrite(PIN_STATUS_LED, HIGH); // [4] Load calibration from NVM loadCalibration(); // I²C Wire.begin(PIN_SDA, PIN_SCL); Wire.setClock(400000); Serial.println(F("[INIT] I2C: 400kHz")); // Sensor detect if (!detectSensor()) { Serial.println(F("[INIT] FATAL: Sensor not found")); transitionTo(State::ERROR_STATE); // Don't start timer — will retry from error state setupAcquisitionTimer(); return; } // Sensor reset if (!resetSensor()) { Serial.println(F("[INIT] FATAL: Reset failed")); transitionTo(State::ERROR_STATE); setupAcquisitionTimer(); return; } delay(10); // Sensor configure if (!configureSensor()) { Serial.println(F("[INIT] FATAL: Configuration failed")); transitionTo(State::ERROR_STATE); setupAcquisitionTimer(); return; } // Initialize state resetProcessingState(); // [1] Start deterministic timer setupAcquisitionTimer(); // [5] Run self-test on boot Serial.println(F("[INIT] Running self-test...")); runVerificationSuite(); // Enter state machine transitionTo(State::INIT); Serial.println(F("[INIT] System ready. Place finger on sensor.")); Serial.println(F("[INIT] Type HELP for commands.")); Serial.println(); } // ============================================================================ // MAIN LOOP — TIMER-DRIVEN // ============================================================================ void loop() { // [1] Wait for timer flag — deterministic timing if (!acquisitionFlag) { // No timer event — handle non-time-critical tasks checkSerialCommands(); return; } // Clear flag and start timing measurement acquisitionFlag = false; uint32_t processingStart = micros(); uint32_t now = millis(); // Handle ERROR state if (currentState == State::ERROR_STATE) { updateStateMachine(); updateStatusLed(); outputResults(); if (currentState == State::INIT) { if (!detectSensor() || !resetSensor() || !configureSensor()) { transitionTo(State::ERROR_STATE); } else { resetProcessingState(); } } recordTimingStats(processingStart); return; } // Read FIFO uint8_t samplesRead = readFIFO(irBuffer, redBuffer, 16); // Process each sample for (uint8_t i = 0; i < samplesRead; i++) { processSample(irBuffer[i], redBuffer[i], now); } // Handle no-data conditions if (samplesRead == 0) { if (lastSuccessfulRead > 0 && (now - lastSuccessfulRead > 2000)) { if (currentState != State::NO_CONTACT && currentState != State::ERROR_STATE) { i2cErrorCount = 11; } } updateStateMachine(); } // Outputs updateStatusLed(); outputResults(); // [1] Periodic diagnostics outputTimingDiagnostics(); // [1] Record timing recordTimingStats(processingStart); // Check serial commands checkSerialCommands(); } A single unified PROMPT that describes what this code is and must do The full working principle (end-to-end, signal to output) A complete functional block diagram (textual + explained) Nothing extra. Nothing missing. 1️⃣ MASTER PROMPT — WHAT THIS CODE IS AND MUST DO You can copy-paste this as a specification or reuse it with another AI or engineer. 🔧 INDUSTRY-GRADE SYSTEM PROMPT Design and implement an industry-grade, non-medical wellness heart-rate monitoring firmware using an ESP32-C3 microcontroller and a MAX30105 optical sensor, connected exclusively over I²C. The system shall: Use only the internal RED and IR LEDs, photodiode, AFE, ADC, and FIFO of the MAX30105 Perform all signal processing digitally on the ESP32-C3 Operate deterministically using a hardware timer interrupt, not delays Detect heartbeats using a Peak-Based Algorithm (PBA) Compute BPM within 40–180 BPM physiological limits Apply rolling average + EMA smoothing Enforce 6-second temporal hysteresis for stable output Quantify signal trustworthiness using a numeric Signal Quality Index (SQI) Adapt LED current dynamically to maintain optimal ADC headroom Use an explicit state machine for lifecycle control Handle errors, contact loss, noise, and sensor faults gracefully Provide manufacturing calibration support via NVM Include self-verification and replay testing Encode regulatory guardrails to prevent medical misuse Output BPM only when confidence and quality conditions are satisfied The firmware must be auditable, deterministic, fault-tolerant, and suitable for pre-production industrial use, but must explicitly disclaim medical usage. 2️⃣ FULL WORKING PRINCIPLE (END-TO-END) This explains how the system actually works, step by step, from photons to numbers. 🟢 STEP 1: Optical Signal Generation (MAX30105) Internal RED and IR LEDs illuminate the skin Light penetrates tissue and is modulated by: blood volume changes (heartbeat) skin tone pressure and perfusion Reflected light is captured by the internal photodiode Key point: No external optics. No analog circuitry outside the chip. 🟡 STEP 2: Analog Front End + ADC (inside MAX30105) Inside the sensor: Photodiode current → Transimpedance amplifier (TIA) Ambient light is cancelled Gain is applied Signal is digitized by internal 18-bit ADC Samples are pushed into the FIFO ESP32 never sees analog signals. 🔵 STEP 3: Deterministic Data Acquisition (ESP32-C3) A hardware timer interrupt fires every 10 ms ISR sets a flag only (no heavy work) Main loop wakes exactly on schedule FIFO is read safely Overflow and I²C faults are detected This guarantees: No jitter accumulation No missed samples Known worst-case timing 🟣 STEP 4: Adaptive LED Current Control Before any heartbeat logic: DC IR level is monitored If signal is too weak → LED current increases If near saturation → LED current decreases Coarse + fine adjustment with settling delay Result: Stable signal amplitude Works across different fingers, skin tones, pressure 🔴 STEP 5: Signal Quality Index (SQI) A numeric confidence score (0.0–1.0) is computed using: Contact quality (DC level range) Perfusion ratio (AC/DC) Signal amplitude Beat-to-beat stability (interval variance) SQI is used to: Gate beat detection Control state transitions Suppress unreliable output This is how industry avoids lying to users. 🟠 STEP 6: Digital Filtering High-pass filter removes DC drift Low-pass filter suppresses noise Combined effect ≈ 0.5–5 Hz band-pass This isolates the heart-rate component. 🟤 STEP 7: Beat Detection (Peak-Based Algorithm) For each filtered sample: Track peaks and valleys Use adaptive threshold Enforce refractory period Validate: amplitude interval (333–1500 ms) Reject noise, double-counts, motion artifacts Each valid beat produces: timestamp interval instant BPM ⚪ STEP 8: BPM Computation and Averaging Instant BPM → rolling average Rolling average → EMA smoothing Calibration offset applied (factory trim) This reduces jitter without hiding real changes. ⚫ STEP 9: 6-Second Temporal Hysteresis (CORE FEATURE) This is output control, not filtering. Logic: Lock onto a reference BPM Continue outputting it while new values stay within ±3 BPM If violated → reset immediately Only accept a new value after 6 consecutive stable seconds Effect: No bouncing numbers No lag when real change happens Predictable, explainable behavior 🔺 STEP 10: State Machine Control Explicit states: INIT NO_CONTACT ACQUIRING TRACKING UNSTABLE ERROR State machine decides: When output is valid When to suppress values When to recover or reset 🔻 STEP 11: Guarded Output (Regulatory Safe) Output rules: Suppress BPM if SQI too low Suppress stale data Prefix output with ~ to indicate approximation Never output physiologically impossible values Warn on extended continuous use This prevents: False confidence Medical misuse Liability exposure 3️⃣ FULL FUNCTIONAL
