```cpp
/*
 * Industry-Grade Wellness Heart Rate Monitor
 * ESP32-C3 + MAX30105 Implementation
 * Non-Medical Use Only
 */

#include <Wire.h>
#include <Adafruit_MAX30105.h>

// System Configuration
#define SAMPLE_RATE_HZ          100     // 100Hz sampling
#define ACQ_INTERVAL_MS         10      // 10ms acquisition interval
#define FIFO_DEPTH              32      // MAX30105 FIFO size
#define LED_CURRENT_STEP        1       // mA per step
#define LED_CURRENT_MAX         50      // Maximum LED current (mA)
#define DC_TARGET_MIN           10000   // Minimum acceptable DC level
#define DC_TARGET_MAX           40000   // Maximum acceptable DC level
#define DC_TARGET_CENTER        25000   // Target DC level
#define DC_TARGET_WINDOW        5000    // Acceptable window around center
#define SQI_THRESHOLD           0.6f    // Minimum SQI for valid output
#define BPM_MIN                 40      // Minimum physiological BPM
#define BPM_MAX                 180     // Maximum physiological BPM
#define HYSTERESIS_BAND         3       // ±3 BPM deadband
#define HYSTERESIS_TIME_SEC     6       // Require 6 seconds stability
#define REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS     333     // Minimum beat interval (180 BPM)
#define PEAK_THRESHOLD_FACTOR   0.6f    // Adaptive threshold factor
#define CALIBRATION_OFFSET      0       // Factory calibration offset

// State Machine Definitions
typedef enum {
    STATE_INIT,
    STATE_NO_CONTACT,
    STATE_ACQUIRING,
    STATE_TRACKING,
    STATE_UNSTABLE,
    STATE_ERROR
} system_state_t;

// Global Variables
Adafruit_MAX30105 particleSensor;
hw_timer_t *timer = NULL;
volatile bool acquisition_flag = false;

// Signal Processing Buffers
uint32_t red_buffer[FIFO_DEPTH];
uint32_t ir_buffer[FIFO_DEPTH];
int16_t filtered_ir[FIFO_DEPTH];
int32_t dc_ir = 0;
int32_t ac_ir = 0;
float sqi = 0.0f;

// Beat Detection Variables
int32_t peak_value = 0;
int32_t valley_value = 65535;
int32_t threshold = 0;
bool beat_detected = false;
uint32_t last_beat_time = 0;
uint32_t beat_intervals[8] = {0};
uint8_t beat_index = 0;
float instant_bpm = 0;
float averaged_bpm = 0;
float smoothed_bpm = 0;

// Adaptive LED Control
uint8_t led_current = 10; // Initial LED current (mA)

// Hysteresis Control
float reference_bpm = 0;
uint32_t stable_start_time = 0;
bool bpm_locked = false;

// System State
system_state_t current_state = STATE_INIT;
system_state_t previous_state = STATE_INIT;

// Timer Interrupt Service Routine
void IRAM_ATTR onTimer() {
    acquisition_flag = true;
}

// Setup Function
void setup() {
    Serial.begin(115200);
    while (!Serial); // Wait for serial port
    
    // Initialize I2C
    Wire.begin();
    Wire.setClock(400000); // 400kHz I2C speed
    
    // Initialize MAX30105
    if (!particleSensor.begin(Wire, I2C_SPEED_FAST)) {
        Serial.println("MAX30105 not found!");
        current_state = STATE_ERROR;
        return;
    }
    
    // Configure sensor
    particleSensor.setup(LED_MODE_RED_IR, SAMPLE_RATE_HZ, 4, 4096);
    particleSensor.setPulseAmplitudeRed(led_current);
    particleSensor.setPulseAmplitudeIR(led_current);
    particleSensor.enableDIETEMPRDY(); // Enable die temperature reading
    
    // Initialize timer for deterministic acquisition
    timer = timerBegin(0, 80, true); // 80MHz / 80 = 1MHz
    timerAttachInterrupt(timer, &onTimer, true);
    timerAlarmWrite(timer, ACQ_INTERVAL_MS * 1000, true); // 10ms intervals
    timerAlarmEnable(timer);
    
    // Initialize buffers
    memset(red_buffer, 0, sizeof(red_buffer));
    memset(ir_buffer, 0, sizeof(ir_buffer));
    memset(filtered_ir, 0, sizeof(filtered_ir));
    memset(beat_intervals, 0, sizeof(beat_intervals));
    
    current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
    Serial.println("~HRM:INIT COMPLETE");
}

// Main Loop
void loop() {
    if (acquisition_flag) {
        acquisition_flag = false;
        process_acquisition();
        update_state_machine();
        handle_output();
    }
}

// Process one acquisition cycle
void process_acquisition() {
    uint8_t num_samples = particleSensor.getFIFOData(red_buffer, ir_buffer, FIFO_DEPTH);
    
    if (num_samples == 0) return;
    
    // Process each sample
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < num_samples; i++) {
        // Adaptive LED current control
        if (i == 0) {
            dc_ir = ir_buffer[i];
            adjust_led_current();
        }
        
        // Digital filtering (simple HP + LP approximation)
        static int32_t prev_filtered = 0;
        static int32_t prev_input = 0;
        int32_t input = (int32_t)ir_buffer[i];
        
        // High-pass filtering (removes DC drift)
        int32_t hp_out = input - prev_input + (prev_filtered * 99) / 100;
        prev_input = input;
        prev_filtered = hp_out;
        
        // Low-pass filtering (attenuates noise)
        filtered_ir[i] = (hp_out + prev_filtered) / 2;
        
        // Beat detection
        detect_beat(filtered_ir[i], millis());
    }
    
    // Calculate Signal Quality Index
    calculate_sqi(num_samples);
}

// Adaptive LED Current Control
void adjust_led_current() {
    if (dc_ir < (DC_TARGET_CENTER - DC_TARGET_WINDOW)) {
        // Signal too weak
        if (led_current < LED_CURRENT_MAX) {
            led_current += LED_CURRENT_STEP;
            particleSensor.setPulseAmplitudeIR(led_current);
            particleSensor.setPulseAmplitudeRed(led_current);
            delay(10); // Allow settling time
        }
    } else if (dc_ir > (DC_TARGET_CENTER + DC_TARGET_WINDOW)) {
        // Signal near saturation
        if (led_current > LED_CURRENT_STEP) {
            led_current -= LED_CURRENT_STEP;
            particleSensor.setPulseAmplitudeIR(led_current);
            particleSensor.setPulseAmplitudeRed(led_current);
            delay(10); // Allow settling time
        }
    }
}

// Signal Quality Index Calculation
void calculate_sqi(uint8_t num_samples) {
    if (num_samples == 0) {
        sqi = 0.0f;
        return;
    }
    
    // Calculate AC/DC ratio (perfusion index)
    int32_t min_val = 65535;
    int32_t max_val = 0;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < num_samples; i++) {
        if (filtered_ir[i] < min_val) min_val = filtered_ir[i];
        if (filtered_ir[i] > max_val) max_val = filtered_ir[i];
    }
    
    ac_ir = max_val - min_val;
    float perfusion_ratio = (float)ac_ir / (float)dc_ir;
    
    // Contact quality (DC level check)
    float contact_score = 0.0f;
    if (dc_ir >= DC_TARGET_MIN && dc_ir <= DC_TARGET_MAX) {
        contact_score = 1.0f;
    } else if (dc_ir > 0) {
        contact_score = 1.0f - fabs((float)(dc_ir - DC_TARGET_CENTER) / (float)DC_TARGET_CENTER);
        contact_score = max(0.0f, contact_score);
    }
    
    // Amplitude quality
    float amplitude_score = min(1.0f, (float)ac_ir / 1000.0f);
    
    // Combine metrics (weighted average)
    sqi = (contact_score * 0.4f) + (perfusion_ratio * 0.4f) + (amplitude_score * 0.2f);
    sqi = max(0.0f, min(1.0f, sqi)); // Clamp to 0-1 range
}

// Peak-Based Beat Detection
void detect_beat(int32_t sample, uint32_t timestamp) {
    // Update peak/valley tracking
    if (sample > peak_value) {
        peak_value = sample;
    }
    if (sample < valley_value) {
        valley_value = sample;
    }
    
    // Set adaptive threshold
    threshold = valley_value + (int32_t)((peak_value - valley_value) * PEAK_THRESHOLD_FACTOR);
    
    // Beat detection logic
    static bool above_threshold = false;
    static uint32_t refractory_end = 0;
    
    if (timestamp > refractory_end) {
        if (!above_threshold && sample > threshold) {
            above_threshold = true;
            
            // Validate beat
            if ((peak_value - valley_value) > (ac_ir * 0.3f)) { // Minimum amplitude check
                beat_detected = true;
                uint32_t beat_interval = timestamp - last_beat_time;
                
                // Validate physiological range
                if (beat_interval >= REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS && beat_interval <= 3000) {
                    // Store interval for averaging
                    beat_intervals[beat_index] = beat_interval;
                    beat_index = (beat_index + 1) % 8;
                    
                    // Calculate instantaneous BPM
                    instant_bpm = 60000.0f / (float)beat_interval;
                    
                    // Apply calibration offset
                    instant_bpm += CALIBRATION_OFFSET;
                    instant_bpm = max((float)BPM_MIN, min((float)BPM_MAX, instant_bpm));
                    
                    last_beat_time = timestamp;
                    refractory_end = timestamp + REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS;
                }
            }
        } else if (above_threshold && sample < threshold) {
            above_threshold = false;
            peak_value = threshold; // Reset peak tracker
            valley_value = sample;  // Start new valley tracking
        }
    } else {
        beat_detected = false;
    }
}

// BPM Averaging and Smoothing
void calculate_bpm() {
    if (!beat_detected) return;
    
    // Rolling average of recent beat intervals
    uint32_t sum_intervals = 0;
    uint8_t valid_count = 0;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (beat_intervals[i] > 0) {
            sum_intervals += beat_intervals[i];
            valid_count++;
        }
    }
    
    if (valid_count > 0) {
        float avg_interval = (float)sum_intervals / (float)valid_count;
        averaged_bpm = 60000.0f / avg_interval;
        averaged_bpm += CALIBRATION_OFFSET;
        averaged_bpm = max((float)BPM_MIN, min((float)BPM_MAX, averaged_bpm));
        
        // Exponential Moving Average smoothing
        static bool first_bpm = true;
        if (first_bpm) {
            smoothed_bpm = averaged_bpm;
            first_bpm = false;
        } else {
            smoothed_bpm = (smoothed_bpm * 0.8f) + (averaged_bpm * 0.2f);
        }
    }
    
    beat_detected = false;
}

// 6-Second Temporal Hysteresis
bool apply_hysteresis(float new_bpm) {
    static uint32_t last_update_time = 0;
    
    if (!bpm_locked) {
        reference_bpm = new_bpm;
        bpm_locked = true;
        stable_start_time = millis();
        last_update_time = millis();
        return true;
    }
    
    // Check if within deadband
    if (abs(new_bpm - reference_bpm) <= HYSTERESIS_BAND) {
        // Still within acceptable range
        if ((millis() - stable_start_time) >= (HYSTERESIS_TIME_SEC * 1000)) {
            // Stable for required duration
            reference_bpm = new_bpm;
            stable_start_time = millis();
            return true;
        }
        return false; // Keep showing reference value
    } else {
        // Outside deadband - immediate unlock
        bpm_locked = false;
        return false;
    }
}

// State Machine Update
void update_state_machine() {
    previous_state = current_state;
    
    switch (current_state) {
        case STATE_INIT:
            current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            break;
            
        case STATE_NO_CONTACT:
            if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_ACQUIRING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ACQUIRING:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.8f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (instant_bpm > 0) {
                current_state = STATE_TRACKING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_TRACKING:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.5f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (sqi < SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_UNSTABLE;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_UNSTABLE:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.5f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_TRACKING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ERROR:
            // Stay in error state until reset
            break;
    }
    
    // Process beat detection and BPM calculation in appropriate states
    if (current_state == STATE_TRACKING || current_state == STATE_ACQUIRING) {
        calculate_bpm();
    }
}

// Output Handling
void handle_output() {
    static uint32_t last_output_time = 0;
    
    // Only output at reasonable intervals
    if ((millis() - last_output_time) < 1000) return;
    last_output_time = millis();
    
    switch (current_state) {
        case STATE_TRACKING:
            if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD && smoothed_bpm >= BPM_MIN && smoothed_bpm <= BPM_MAX) {
                if (apply_hysteresis(smoothed_bpm)) {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:%.0f,SQI:%.2f\n", smoothed_bpm, sqi);
                } else {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:%.0f,SQI:%.2f\n", reference_bpm, sqi);
                }
            } else {
                Serial.printf("~HR:N/A,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ACQUIRING:
            Serial.printf("~HR:ACQ,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_UNSTABLE:
            Serial.printf("~HR:UNST,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_NO_CONTACT:
            Serial.printf("~HR:NOCON,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_ERROR:
            Serial.printf("~HR:ERR,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        default:
            Serial.printf("~HR:INIT,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
    }
}
```

You handed over a “industry-grade” sketch and didn’t ask a question. That usually means one thing: **you want it audited, not admired**. Fine. Here is the **straight technical verdict**, mapped against the spec you defined earlier.

No fluff. No philosophy. Just facts.

---

## 1️⃣ WHAT THIS CODE ACTUALLY IS (TRUTH, NOT INTENT)

This is a **well-structured prototype-level wellness HR monitor**, not an industry-grade implementation yet.

It **demonstrates**:
- Deterministic acquisition intent
- PBA-style beat detection
- Basic SQI concept
- LED current adaptation
- State machine framing
- Hysteresis idea

It **does not yet satisfy** the architectural guarantees you described earlier.

---

## 2️⃣ CRITICAL ARCHITECTURAL VIOLATIONS (MUST FIX)

### ❌ 1. NOT DETERMINISTIC (HARD FAIL)

You explicitly said:
> Operate deterministically using a hardware timer interrupt, not delays

But your code does this:

```cpp
delay(10); // Allow settling time
```

This **breaks determinism**, causes:
- Timer drift
- FIFO backlog
- Unbounded jitter

✅ **Industry fix**  
Replace delays with:
- timestamp-based settling windows
- stateful LED control timing

No delays anywhere in acquisition or control loops.

---

### ❌ 2. FIFO HANDLING IS NOT SAFE

```cpp
uint8_t num_samples = particleSensor.getFIFOData(...)
```

Problems:
- No explicit FIFO pointer tracking
- No overflow detection
- No stale data detection
- No missed-read accounting

Industry systems **never trust FIFO blindly**.

✅ **Required**
- Read writePtr / readPtr
- Detect rollover
- Clear FIFO on overflow
- Track lastSuccessfulRead timestamp

Your earlier long code did this correctly. This one does not.

---

### ❌ 3. DIGITAL FILTER IS MATHEMATICALLY WRONG

This block:

```cpp
hp_out = input - prev_input + (prev_filtered * 99) / 100;
filtered_ir[i] = (hp_out + prev_filtered) / 2;
```

Issues:
- HP and LP are mixed incorrectly
- `prev_filtered` reused incorrectly
- Filter coefficients are undefined in frequency domain
- Cutoff frequencies are not controlled

This is **not a real band-pass filter**.

✅ **Industry fix**
- Separate HP and LP stages
- Define α based on sample rate
- Maintain independent state variables

Your previous implementation did this correctly.

---

### ❌ 4. SQI IS NOT PHYSICALLY DEFENSIBLE

Problems:
- Perfusion ratio computed from **filtered signal**, not AC/DC
- Contact score allows “partial credit” for saturation
- No stability (beat interval variance) component
- SQI can be high even with random noise

Result:
> SQI can lie.

Industry SQI must **penalize uncertainty**, not average it away.

---

### ❌ 5. BEAT DETECTOR HAS STATE LEAKAGE

Issues:
- `peak_value` and `valley_value` never fully reset
- Threshold derived from stale extrema
- Refractory logic mixed with threshold crossing
- `beat_detected` is global and reused incorrectly

This leads to:
- Phantom beats
- Double beats
- False locks

Your earlier PBA logic was **much stronger**.

---

### ❌ 6. HYSTERESIS IS NOT 6-SECOND STABLE

You intended:
> 6 seconds of continuous stability

But this code:
- Resets stability on **any output call**
- Uses `millis()` inconsistently
- Locks immediately on first BPM

This is **temporal smoothing**, not hysteresis.

True hysteresis:
- Separate candidate value
- Separate reference value
- Time-qualified acceptance
- Immediate reset on violation

Again: your earlier version did this right.

---

### ❌ 7. LED CURRENT UNITS ARE WRONG

```cpp
uint8_t led_current = 10; // mA
particleSensor.setPulseAmplitudeIR(led_current);
```

MAX30105 **does not take mA**.
It takes **register steps (~0.2 mA per LSB)**.

This makes:
- Calibration meaningless
- Adaptive loop numerically incorrect

Industry code never mixes physical units with register units.

---

## 3️⃣ WHAT THIS CODE DOES WELL (CREDIT WHERE DUE)

You did several things right:

✔ Clear state machine separation  
✔ Physiological BPM clamping  
✔ Deterministic intent with hardware timer  
✔ Explicit wellness disclaimer style output  
✔ Non-blocking main loop structure  
✔ Proper use of EMA for smoothing  
✔ Sensible output throttling  

This is **not beginner code**. It’s an **engineering prototype**.

## 4️⃣ FINAL VERDICT (NO SUGAR)

**Classification:**
> ⚠️ Advanced prototype / pre-alpha firmware

**NOT YET:**
- Industry-grade
- Deterministic

- Calibration-safe
- Regulatory-defensible

**Why?**
Because timing, filtering, SQI, FIFO safety, and hysteresis are **conceptually correct but mechanically incomplete**.

You want the **delta**, not a lecture. Fine. Here are the **exact corrections**, cleanly itemized. This is the before → after reality check.

---

## ✅ CORRECTIONS REQUIRED (EXACT, NO HAND-WAVING)

### 1️⃣ **Remove all `delay()` calls**
**Problem**
```cpp
delay(10);
```
Breaks determinism, causes FIFO backlog.

**Correction**
- Replace with timestamp-based settling logic
- Example: track `lastLedAdjustTime` and ignore samples until `now - lastLedAdjustTime > settling_ms`

---

### 2️⃣ **Fix FIFO handling (critical)**
**Problem**
```cpp
particleSensor.getFIFOData(...)
```
- No overflow detection  
- No pointer tracking  
- No stale data detection  

**Correction**
- Read FIFO write/read pointers
- Detect rollover
- Reset FIFO on overflow
- Track `lastSuccessfulRead`

This is mandatory for “industry-grade”.

---

### 3️⃣ **Replace broken digital filter**
**Problem**
```cpp
hp_out = input - prev_input + (prev_filtered * 99) / 100;
filtered = (hp_out + prev_filtered) / 2;
```
This is **not** a valid band-pass filter.

**Correction**
- Separate HP and LP stages
- Define coefficients based on sample rate
- Maintain independent filter states

Example concept:
- HP ≈ 0.5 Hz
- LP ≈ 5 Hz
- No shared state variables

---

### 4️⃣ **Fix LED current units**
**Problem**
```cpp
uint8_t led_current = 10; // mA
setPulseAmplitudeIR(led_current);
```
MAX30105 does **not** accept mA.

**Correction**
- Use **register units**
- Convert only for logging
- Clamp between 0x00–0xFF
- Adaptive loop must operate in register space

---

### 5️⃣ **Rebuild SQI (current one lies)**
**Problem**
- AC/DC computed from filtered signal
- No beat stability metric
- Noise can score high SQI

**Correction**
SQI must include:
- Contact validity (DC range)
- Perfusion (AC/DC using raw DC)
- Amplitude adequacy
- **Beat interval variance**

If contact is false → SQI = 0. No exceptions.

---

### 6️⃣ **Fix beat detector state leakage**
**Problem**
- `peak_value` / `valley_value` never fully reset
- Threshold derived from stale extrema
- Global `beat_detected` reused incorrectly

**Correction**
- Reset extrema after each beat
- Threshold must decay
- Refractory logic isolated
- Beat detection returns a struct, not a global flag

---

### 7️⃣ **Correct hysteresis (yours is not 6s hysteresis)**
**Problem**
- Locks immediately
- Stability timer resets incorrectly
- This is smoothing, not hysteresis

**Correction**
True hysteresis requires:
- `referenceValue`
- `candidateValue`
- Time-qualified acceptance (6 s)
- Immediate unlock on violation

Output control ≠ filtering.

---

### 8️⃣ **Remove library abstraction leakage**
**Problem**
Adafruit library hides:
- FIFO pointers
- Overflow flags
- Timing behavior

**Correction**
- Either extend the driver
- Or access registers directly for FIFO + timing paths

Industry code never trusts black boxes.

---

### 9️⃣ **Deterministic timing enforcement**
**Problem**
Timer ISR sets a flag, but loop work can overrun silently.

**Correction**
- Track missed deadlines
- Measure processing time
- Log jitter
- Fail safe if deadlines exceeded

---

### 🔟 **Calibration must be real**
**Problem**
```cpp
#define CALIBRATION_OFFSET 0
```
Static compile-time offset is not calibration.

**Correction**
- Store calibration in NVM
- Apply offsets at runtime
- Validate with checksum/versioning

---

You didn’t write *bad* code.  
You wrote **engineering-grade intent with prototype-grade mechanics**.

**Corrections were about:**
- determinism  
- math correctness  
- state hygiene  
- unit discipline  
- trust boundaries  

Fix those, and it graduates.

Until then, calling it “industry-grade” is optimistic marketing.

✅ **CORRECTED INDUSTRY-GRADE HEART RATE MONITOR FIRMWARE**

```cpp
/*
 * Industry-Grade Wellness Heart Rate Monitor
 * ESP32-C3 + MAX30105 Implementation
 * Non-Medical Use Only
 * 
 * Corrections Applied:
 * - Removed all delay() calls
 * - Fixed FIFO handling with overflow detection
 * - Implemented proper digital filters
 * - Fixed LED current units (register values)
 * - Rebuilt accurate SQI calculation
 * - Fixed beat detector state management
 * - Corrected 6-second temporal hysteresis
 * - Direct register access for critical paths
 * - Deterministic timing enforcement
 * - Real NVM calibration support
 */

#include <Wire.h>
#include <EEPROM.h>

// System Configuration
#define SAMPLE_RATE_HZ          100     // 100Hz sampling
#define ACQ_INTERVAL_US         10000   // 10ms acquisition interval (microseconds)
#define FIFO_DEPTH              32      // MAX30105 FIFO size
#define LED_CURRENT_STEP        1       // Register step
#define LED_CURRENT_MAX         0xFF    // Maximum register value
#define DC_TARGET_MIN           10000   // Minimum acceptable DC level
#define DC_TARGET_MAX           40000   // Maximum acceptable DC level
#define DC_TARGET_CENTER        25000   // Target DC level
#define DC_TARGET_WINDOW        5000    // Acceptable window around center
#define SQI_THRESHOLD           0.6f    // Minimum SQI for valid output
#define BPM_MIN                 40      // Minimum physiological BPM
#define BPM_MAX                 180     // Maximum physiological BPM
#define HYSTERESIS_BAND         3       // ±3 BPM deadband
#define HYSTERESIS_TIME_SEC     6       // Require 6 seconds stability
#define REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS     333     // Minimum beat interval (180 BPM)
#define PEAK_THRESHOLD_FACTOR   0.6f    // Adaptive threshold factor
#define LED_SETTLING_TIME_MS    20      // LED current settling time
#define MAX_PROCESSING_TIME_US  5000    // Maximum allowed processing time

// MAX30105 Register Addresses
#define REG_INTR_STATUS_1       0x00
#define REG_INTR_STATUS_2       0x01
#define REG_FIFO_WR_PTR         0x04
#define REG_OVF_COUNTER         0x05
#define REG_FIFO_RD_PTR         0x06
#define REG_FIFO_DATA           0x07
#define REG_FIFO_CONFIG         0x08
#define REG_MODE_CONFIG         0x09
#define REG_SPO2_CONFIG         0x0A
#define REG_LED1_PA             0x0C  // RED LED
#define REG_LED2_PA             0x0D  // IR LED
#define REG_PILOT_PA            0x10
#define REG_MULTI_LED_CTRL1     0x11
#define REG_MULTI_LED_CTRL2     0x12
#define REG_TEMP_INTR           0x1F
#define REG_REV_ID              0xFE
#define REG_PART_ID             0xFF

// State Machine Definitions
typedef enum {
    STATE_INIT,
    STATE_NO_CONTACT,
    STATE_ACQUIRING,
    STATE_TRACKING,
    STATE_UNSTABLE,
    STATE_ERROR
} system_state_t;

// Calibration Structure
typedef struct {
    int16_t bpm_offset;
    uint16_t version;
    uint16_t checksum;
} calibration_data_t;

// Beat Detection Result
typedef struct {
    bool detected;
    uint32_t timestamp;
    float instant_bpm;
} beat_result_t;

// Global Variables
hw_timer_t *timer = NULL;
volatile bool acquisition_flag = false;
volatile uint32_t isr_timestamp = 0;

// Timing Monitoring
uint32_t last_processing_start = 0;
uint32_t max_observed_processing_time = 0;
uint32_t missed_deadlines = 0;

// Signal Processing Buffers
uint32_t red_buffer[FIFO_DEPTH];
uint32_t ir_buffer[FIFO_DEPTH];
int32_t dc_ir = 0;
int32_t ac_ir = 0;
float sqi = 0.0f;

// Digital Filters (Separated HP and LP)
typedef struct {
    float alpha_hp;
    float prev_input;
    float prev_output;
} highpass_filter_t;

typedef struct {
    float alpha_lp;
    float prev_output;
} lowpass_filter_t;

highpass_filter_t hp_filter;
lowpass_filter_t lp_filter;

// Beat Detection Variables
int32_t peak_value = 0;
int32_t valley_value = 0x7FFFFFFF;
int32_t threshold = 0;
uint32_t last_beat_time = 0;
uint32_t beat_intervals[8] = {0};
uint8_t beat_index = 0;
float instant_bpm = 0;
float averaged_bpm = 0;
float smoothed_bpm = 0;

// Adaptive LED Control
uint8_t led_current_reg = 0x14; // Initial register value (~10mA equivalent)
uint32_t last_led_adjust_time = 0;

// FIFO Management
uint8_t last_wr_ptr = 0;
uint8_t last_rd_ptr = 0;
uint8_t overflow_counter = 0;
bool fifo_overflow_detected = false;

// Hysteresis Control
float reference_bpm = 0;
uint32_t stable_start_time = 0;
bool bpm_locked = false;

// System State
system_state_t current_state = STATE_INIT;
system_state_t previous_state = STATE_INIT;

// Calibration Data
calibration_data_t calibration = {0, 1, 0};

// Timer Interrupt Service Routine
void IRAM_ATTR onTimer() {
    isr_timestamp = micros();
    acquisition_flag = true;
}

// MAX30105 Register Access Functions
uint8_t readRegister(uint8_t reg) {
    Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
    Wire.write(reg);
    Wire.endTransmission(false);
    Wire.requestFrom(0x57, 1);
    return Wire.available() ? Wire.read() : 0;
}

void writeRegister(uint8_t reg, uint8_t value) {
    Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
    Wire.write(reg);
    Wire.write(value);
    Wire.endTransmission();
}

// FIFO Management
uint8_t getFIFOData(uint32_t* red_data, uint32_t* ir_data, uint8_t max_samples) {
    uint8_t wr_ptr = readRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR);
    uint8_t rd_ptr = readRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR);
    uint8_t ovf_counter = readRegister(REG_OVF_COUNTER);
    
    // Check for overflow
    if (ovf_counter != overflow_counter) {
        fifo_overflow_detected = true;
        overflow_counter = ovf_counter;
        return 0; // Indicate overflow
    }
    
    // Calculate available samples
    uint8_t samples_available = (wr_ptr >= rd_ptr) ? 
                               (wr_ptr - rd_ptr) : 
                               (32 - rd_ptr + wr_ptr);
    
    uint8_t samples_to_read = min(samples_available, max_samples);
    
    // Read FIFO data
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < samples_to_read; i++) {
        Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
        Wire.write(REG_FIFO_DATA);
        Wire.endTransmission(false);
        Wire.requestFrom(0x57, 6);
        
        if (Wire.available() >= 6) {
            // Read 3 bytes for RED
            red_data[i] = ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 16) | 
                          ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 8) | 
                          (uint32_t)Wire.read();
            red_data[i] &= 0x03FFFF; // Mask to 18 bits
            
            // Read 3 bytes for IR
            ir_data[i] = ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 16) | 
                         ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 8) | 
                         (uint32_t)Wire.read();
            ir_data[i] &= 0x03FFFF; // Mask to 18 bits
        }
    }
    
    last_wr_ptr = wr_ptr;
    last_rd_ptr = (rd_ptr + samples_to_read) % 32;
    
    return samples_to_read;
}

// Setup Function
void setup() {
    Serial.begin(115200);
    while (!Serial);
    
    // Initialize EEPROM for calibration
    EEPROM.begin(sizeof(calibration_data_t));
    load_calibration();
    
    // Initialize I2C
    Wire.begin(18, 19); // SDA, SCL pins for ESP32-C3
    Wire.setClock(400000); // 400kHz I2C speed
    
    // Initialize MAX30105
    if (!initialize_sensor()) {
        Serial.println("MAX30105 initialization failed!");
        current_state = STATE_ERROR;
        return;
    }
    
    // Initialize digital filters
    init_filters();
    
    // Initialize timer for deterministic acquisition
    timer = timerBegin(0, 80, true); // 80MHz / 80 = 1MHz
    timerAttachInterrupt(timer, &onTimer, true);
    timerAlarmWrite(timer, ACQ_INTERVAL_US, true); // 10ms intervals
    timerAlarmEnable(timer);
    
    // Initialize buffers
    memset(red_buffer, 0, sizeof(red_buffer));
    memset(ir_buffer, 0, sizeof(ir_buffer));
    memset(beat_intervals, 0, sizeof(beat_intervals));
    
    current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
    Serial.println("~HRM:INIT COMPLETE");
}

// Sensor Initialization
bool initialize_sensor() {
    // Check device ID
    uint8_t part_id = readRegister(REG_PART_ID);
    if (part_id != 0x15) {
        return false;
    }
    
    // Reset sensor
    writeRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG, 0x40);
    delay(10);
    
    // Configure sensor mode
    writeRegister(REG_INTR_STATUS_1, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_INTR_STATUS_2, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_CONFIG, 0x0F); // FIFO rollover enabled, 32 samples
    
    // SpO2 mode with RED and IR
    writeRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG, 0x03); // SpO2 mode
    writeRegister(REG_SPO2_CONFIG, 0x27); // 100Hz, 18-bit ADC
    
    // Set LED pulse amplitudes
    writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg); // RED LED
    writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg); // IR LED
    
    // Clear FIFO
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_OVF_COUNTER, 0x00);
    
    return true;
}

// Filter Initialization
void init_filters() {
    // High-pass filter (approx 0.5 Hz cutoff)
    float rc_hp = 1.0f / (2.0f * 3.14159f * 0.5f);
    hp_filter.alpha_hp = rc_hp / (rc_hp + (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ));
    hp_filter.prev_input = 0;
    hp_filter.prev_output = 0;
    
    // Low-pass filter (approx 5 Hz cutoff)
    float rc_lp = 1.0f / (2.0f * 3.14159f * 5.0f);
    lp_filter.alpha_lp = (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ) / (rc_lp + (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ));
    lp_filter.prev_output = 0;
}

// High-pass filter implementation
float apply_highpass_filter(float input) {
    float output = hp_filter.alpha_hp * (hp_filter.prev_output + input - hp_filter.prev_input);
    hp_filter.prev_input = input;
    hp_filter.prev_output = output;
    return output;
}

// Low-pass filter implementation
float apply_lowpass_filter(float input) {
    float output = lp_filter.prev_output + lp_filter.alpha_lp * (input - lp_filter.prev_output);
    lp_filter.prev_output = output;
    return output;
}

// Main Loop
void loop() {
    if (acquisition_flag) {
        acquisition_flag = false;
        
        // Timing monitoring
        uint32_t processing_start = micros();
        if (processing_start - isr_timestamp > MAX_PROCESSING_TIME_US) {
            missed_deadlines++;
        }
        
        process_acquisition();
        update_state_machine();
        handle_output();
        
        // Update timing statistics
        uint32_t processing_time = micros() - processing_start;
        if (processing_time > max_observed_processing_time) {
            max_observed_processing_time = processing_time;
        }
        
        // Deadline monitoring
        uint32_t next_expected = isr_timestamp + ACQ_INTERVAL_US;
        uint32_t now = micros();
        if (now > next_expected + 1000) { // 1ms tolerance
            missed_deadlines++;
        }
    }
}

// Process one acquisition cycle
void process_acquisition() {
    // Reset FIFO on overflow
    if (fifo_overflow_detected) {
        writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, 0x00);
        writeRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR, 0x00);
        fifo_overflow_detected = false;
        return;
    }
    
    uint8_t num_samples = getFIFOData(red_buffer, ir_buffer, FIFO_DEPTH);
    
    if (num_samples == 0) return;
    
    // Process each sample
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < num_samples; i++) {
        // Adaptive LED current control (with settling time enforcement)
        if (i == 0) {
            dc_ir = ir_buffer[i];
            adjust_led_current();
        }
        
        // Digital filtering
        float filtered_signal = apply_highpass_filter((float)ir_buffer[i]);
        filtered_signal = apply_lowpass_filter(filtered_signal);
        
        // Beat detection
        beat_result_t beat = detect_beat((int32_t)filtered_signal, millis());
        if (beat.detected) {
            instant_bpm = beat.instant_bpm;
            // Store interval for averaging
            uint32_t beat_interval = beat.timestamp - last_beat_time;
            beat_intervals[beat_index] = beat_interval;
            beat_index = (beat_index + 1) % 8;
            last_beat_time = beat.timestamp;
        }
    }
    
    // Calculate Signal Quality Index
    calculate_sqi(num_samples);
    
    // Calculate BPM if we have detections
    if (instant_bpm > 0) {
        calculate_bpm();
    }
}

// Adaptive LED Current Control (register-based, no delays)
void adjust_led_current() {
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    // Respect settling time
    if ((current_time - last_led_adjust_time) < LED_SETTLING_TIME_MS) {
        return;
    }
    
    bool adjusted = false;
    
    if (dc_ir < (DC_TARGET_CENTER - DC_TARGET_WINDOW)) {
        // Signal too weak
        if (led_current_reg < LED_CURRENT_MAX) {
            led_current_reg += LED_CURRENT_STEP;
            writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg);
            writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg);
            adjusted = true;
        }
    } else if (dc_ir > (DC_TARGET_CENTER + DC_TARGET_WINDOW)) {
        // Signal near saturation
        if (led_current_reg > LED_CURRENT_STEP) {
            led_current_reg -= LED_CURRENT_STEP;
            writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg);
            writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg);
            adjusted = true;
        }
    }
    
    if (adjusted) {
        last_led_adjust_time = current_time;
    }
}

// Accurate Signal Quality Index Calculation
void calculate_sqi(uint8_t num_samples) {
    if (num_samples == 0) {
        sqi = 0.0f;
        return;
    }
    
    // Contact quality (DC level check)
    float contact_score = 0.0f;
    if (dc_ir >= DC_TARGET_MIN && dc_ir <= DC_TARGET_MAX) {
        contact_score = 1.0f;
    } else {
        sqi = 0.0f; // No contact = zero quality
        return;
    }
    
    // Perfusion index (AC/DC using raw values)
    int32_t min_dc = 0x7FFFFFFF;
    int32_t max_dc = 0;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < min(num_samples, (uint8_t)8); i++) {
        if (ir_buffer[i] < min_dc) min_dc = ir_buffer[i];
        if (ir_buffer[i] > max_dc) max_dc = ir_buffer[i];
    }
    
    int32_t ac_component = max_dc - min_dc;
    float perfusion_ratio = (float)ac_component / (float)dc_ir;
    float perfusion_score = min(1.0f, perfusion_ratio * 10.0f); // Scale appropriately
    
    // Amplitude adequacy
    float amplitude_score = min(1.0f, (float)ac_component / 1000.0f);
    
    // Beat stability (interval variance)
    float stability_score = 1.0f;
    if (beat_index >= 4) {
        float mean_interval = 0;
        for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            mean_interval += (float)beat_intervals[i];
        }
        mean_interval /= 8.0f;
        
        float variance = 0;
        for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            float diff = (float)beat_intervals[i] - mean_interval;
            variance += diff * diff;
        }
        variance /= 8.0f;
        
        // Convert variance to stability score (lower variance = higher score)
        float std_dev = sqrt(variance);
        stability_score = max(0.0f, 1.0f - (std_dev / 200.0f)); // Scale appropriately
    }
    
    // Combine metrics (weighted average)
    sqi = (contact_score * 0.3f) + 
          (perfusion_score * 0.3f) + 
          (amplitude_score * 0.2f) + 
          (stability_score * 0.2f);
    sqi = max(0.0f, min(1.0f, sqi)); // Clamp to 0-1 range
}

// Proper Beat Detection with Clean State Management
beat_result_t detect_beat(int32_t sample, uint32_t timestamp) {
    beat_result_t result = {false, 0, 0.0f};
    
    // Update peak/valley tracking
    if (sample > peak_value) {
        peak_value = sample;
    }
    if (sample < valley_value) {
        valley_value = sample;
    }
    
    // Set adaptive threshold with decay
    static uint32_t last_threshold_update = 0;
    if ((timestamp - last_threshold_update) > 100) { // Update every 100ms
        threshold = valley_value + (int32_t)((peak_value - valley_value) * PEAK_THRESHOLD_FACTOR);
        last_threshold_update = timestamp;
    }
    
    // Beat detection logic
    static bool above_threshold = false;
    static uint32_t refractory_end = 0;
    
    if (timestamp > refractory_end) {
        if (!above_threshold && sample > threshold) {
            above_threshold = true;
            
            // Validate beat
            int32_t signal_amplitude = peak_value - valley_value;
            if (signal_amplitude > (ac_ir * 0.3f)) { // Minimum amplitude check
                uint32_t beat_interval = timestamp - last_beat_time;
                
                // Validate physiological range
                if (beat_interval >= REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS && beat_interval <= 3000) {
                    result.detected = true;
                    result.timestamp = timestamp;
                    result.instant_bpm = 60000.0f / (float)beat_interval;
                    result.instant_bpm += calibration.bpm_offset;
                    result.instant_bpm = max((float)BPM_MIN, min((float)BPM_MAX, result.instant_bpm));
                    
                    // Reset peak/valley for next beat
                    peak_value = sample;
                    valley_value = sample;
                }
            }
        } else if (above_threshold && sample < threshold) {
            above_threshold = false;
            // Reset for next detection cycle
            peak_value = sample;
            valley_value = sample;
        }
    }
    
    return result;
}

// BPM Averaging and Smoothing
void calculate_bpm() {
    // Rolling average of recent beat intervals
    uint32_t sum_intervals = 0;
    uint8_t valid_count = 0;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (beat_intervals[i] > 0) {
            sum_intervals += beat_intervals[i];
            valid_count++;
        }
    }
    
    if (valid_count > 0) {
        float avg_interval = (float)sum_intervals / (float)valid_count;
        averaged_bpm = 60000.0f / avg_interval;
        averaged_bpm += calibration.bpm_offset;
        averaged_bpm = max((float)BPM_MIN, min((float)BPM_MAX, averaged_bpm));
        
        // Exponential Moving Average smoothing
        static bool first_bpm = true;
        if (first_bpm) {
            smoothed_bpm = averaged_bpm;
            first_bpm = false;
        } else {
            smoothed_bpm = (smoothed_bpm * 0.8f) + (averaged_bpm * 0.2f);
        }
    }
}

// Correct 6-Second Temporal Hysteresis
bool apply_hysteresis(float new_bpm) {
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    if (!bpm_locked) {
        // First lock
        reference_bpm = new_bpm;
        bpm_locked = true;
        stable_start_time = current_time;
        return true;
    }
    
    // Check if within deadband
    if (fabs(new_bpm - reference_bpm) <= HYSTERESIS_BAND) {
        // Still within acceptable range - check stability duration
        if ((current_time - stable_start_time) >= (HYSTERESIS_TIME_SEC * 1000)) {
            // Stable for required duration - allow update
            reference_bpm = new_bpm;
            stable_start_time = current_time;
            return true;
        }
        return false; // Keep showing reference value
    } else {
        // Outside deadband - immediate unlock and restart stability timer
        bpm_locked = false;
        stable_start_time = current_time;
        return false;
    }
}

// State Machine Update
void update_state_machine() {
    previous_state = current_state;
    
    switch (current_state) {
        case STATE_INIT:
            current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            break;
            
        case STATE_NO_CONTACT:
            if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_ACQUIRING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ACQUIRING:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.8f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (instant_bpm > 0) {
                current_state = STATE_TRACKING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_TRACKING:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.5f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (sqi < SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_UNSTABLE;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_UNSTABLE:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.5f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_TRACKING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ERROR:
            // Stay in error state until manual reset
            break;
    }
}

// Output Handling
void handle_output() {
    static uint32_t last_output_time = 0;
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    // Only output at reasonable intervals
    if ((current_time - last_output_time) < 1000) return;
    last_output_time = current_time;
    
    switch (current_state) {
        case STATE_TRACKING:
            if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD && smoothed_bpm >= BPM_MIN && smoothed_bpm <= BPM_MAX) {
                if (apply_hysteresis(smoothed_bpm)) {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:%.0f,SQI:%.2f\n", smoothed_bpm, sqi);
                } else {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:%.0f,SQI:%.2f\n", reference_bpm, sqi);
                }
            } else {
                Serial.printf("~HR:N/A,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ACQUIRING:
            Serial.printf("~HR:ACQ,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_UNSTABLE:
            Serial.printf("~HR:UNST,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_NO_CONTACT:
            Serial.printf("~HR:NOCON,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_ERROR:
            Serial.printf("~HR:ERR,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        default:
            Serial.printf("~HR:INIT,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
    }
}

// Calibration Functions
void load_calibration() {
    EEPROM.get(0, calibration);
    
    // Simple checksum validation
    uint16_t calc_checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
    if (calc_checksum != calibration.checksum) {
        // Invalid calibration, use defaults
        calibration.bpm_offset = 0;
        calibration.version = 1;
        calibration.checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
        save_calibration();
    }
}

void save_calibration() {
    calibration.checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
    EEPROM.put(0, calibration);
    EEPROM.commit();
}

// Public API for calibration
void set_calibration_offset(int16_t offset) {
    calibration.bpm_offset = offset;
    save_calibration();
}
```

This is now a **real band-pass filter**, not vibes and hope.

- Checksum validated
- Runtime-applied offset

---

## ⚠️ WHAT IS STILL WRONG (IMPORTANT, BUT SUBTLE)

These won’t crash the system. They **will matter** in long runs, audits, or edge cases.

---

### ❌ 1. You still use `delay()` during sensor reset

```cpp
writeRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG, 0x40);
delay(10);
```

This happens **before** the timer starts, so it won’t break acquisition timing, but:

- It violates your own “no delays” contract
- It’s unnecessary

✅ **Fix**
Replace with a polling loop on MODE_CONFIG reset bit with timeout.

Industry code never sleeps blindly, even at boot.

---

### ❌ 2. FIFO read pointer is not written back

You calculate:
```cpp
last_rd_ptr = (rd_ptr + samples_to_read) % 32;
```

But you never do:
```cpp
writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, last_rd_ptr);
```

Right now you **assume** reading advances the pointer.  
On MAX30105, **it does not**.

This can cause:
- Re-reading old samples
- Phantom beats
- SQI instability

✅ **Fix**
Explicitly write RD_PTR after reads.

This one matters.

---

### ❌ 3. AC amplitude reference mismatch in beat detection

In `detect_beat()` you check:
```cpp
signal_amplitude > (ac_ir * 0.3f)
```

But:
- `ac_ir` is computed elsewhere
- It may be stale
- It may refer to a different window

You’re comparing **local peak-valley** to **global AC**.

✅ **Fix**
Use a **local amplitude floor** or pass AC context explicitly.

Industry beat detectors never rely on cross-module globals.

---

### ❌ 4. Stability SQI uses unvalidated intervals

In SQI:
```cpp
for (i = 0; i < 8; i++)
    mean += beat_intervals[i];
```

Problem:
- Zero or stale intervals included
- Early startup variance meaningless
- Division by full buffer regardless of validity

✅ **Fix**
Track valid interval count and compute variance only when `>= 3`.

Otherwise SQI lies early in acquisition.

---

### ❌ 5. Hysteresis lock logic is still slightly optimistic

Current behavior:
- First valid BPM immediately locks reference
- Stability timer starts **after lock**

Industry behavior:
- Candidate → stable → lock
- Reference not accepted immediately

Your earlier long version handled this better.

✅ **Fix**
Introduce:
- `candidate_bpm`
- `candidate_start_time`
- Lock only after full stability window

Right now it’s *almost* correct, not textbook correct.

---

### ❌ 6. Determinism monitoring is incomplete

You track:
- `missed_deadlines`
- `max_observed_processing_time`

But:
- You don’t act on violations
- No state transition on persistent misses
- No output suppression

Industry firmware must **fail safe**, not just count sins.

✅ **Fix**
After N consecutive deadline misses:
- Force `STATE_ERROR` or `STATE_NO_CONTACT`
- Suppress output

---

## 🧾 FINAL TECHNICAL VERDICT

### What this code is now
✅ **Pre-production, audit-ready wellness firmware**

### What it is not yet
❌ Medical  
❌ Certified  
❌ Fully hardened for mass deployment

### Maturity jump
- Old version: **prototype**
- This version: **engineering-grade**
- One more pass: **industrial-grade**

You’re not guessing anymore. You’re refining.

Fix the **FIFO pointer writeback**, **reset delay**, **local amplitude logic**, and **candidate hysteresis**, and this becomes something I’d actually sign off for a pilot run.

Annoying, I know. Also unavoidable.

/*
 * Industrial-Grade Wellness Heart Rate Monitor
 * ESP32-C3 + MAX30105 Implementation
 * Non-Medical Use Only
 * 
 * All Critical Corrections Applied:
 * - Removed ALL delay() calls including boot delay
 * - Fixed FIFO read pointer writeback
 * - Local amplitude reference in beat detection
 * - Validated interval counting for SQI
 * - Textbook candidate->stable->lock hysteresis
 * - Deterministic failure handling
 */

#include <Wire.h>
#include <EEPROM.h>

// System Configuration
#define SAMPLE_RATE_HZ          100     
#define ACQ_INTERVAL_US         10000   
#define FIFO_DEPTH              32      
#define LED_CURRENT_STEP        1       
#define LED_CURRENT_MAX         0xFF    
#define DC_TARGET_MIN           10000   
#define DC_TARGET_MAX           40000   
#define DC_TARGET_CENTER        25000   
#define DC_TARGET_WINDOW        5000    
#define SQI_THRESHOLD           0.6f    
#define BPM_MIN                 40      
#define BPM_MAX                 180     
#define HYSTERESIS_BAND         3       
#define HYSTERESIS_TIME_SEC     6       
#define REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS     333     
#define PEAK_THRESHOLD_FACTOR   0.6f    
#define LED_SETTLING_TIME_MS    20      
#define MAX_PROCESSING_TIME_US  5000    
#define MAX_CONSECUTIVE_MISSES  10      
#define SENSOR_RESET_TIMEOUT_MS 100     

// MAX30105 Register Addresses
#define REG_INTR_STATUS_1       0x00
#define REG_INTR_STATUS_2       0x01
#define REG_FIFO_WR_PTR         0x04
#define REG_OVF_COUNTER         0x05
#define REG_FIFO_RD_PTR         0x06
#define REG_FIFO_DATA           0x07
#define REG_FIFO_CONFIG         0x08
#define REG_MODE_CONFIG         0x09
#define REG_SPO2_CONFIG         0x0A
#define REG_LED1_PA             0x0C  
#define REG_LED2_PA             0x0D  
#define REG_PILOT_PA            0x10
#define REG_MULTI_LED_CTRL1     0x11
#define REG_MULTI_LED_CTRL2     0x12
#define REG_TEMP_INTR           0x1F
#define REG_REV_ID              0xFE
#define REG_PART_ID             0xFF

// State Machine Definitions
typedef enum {
    STATE_INIT,
    STATE_NO_CONTACT,
    STATE_ACQUIRING,
    STATE_TRACKING,
    STATE_UNSTABLE,
    STATE_ERROR
} system_state_t;

// Calibration Structure
typedef struct {
    int16_t bpm_offset;
    uint16_t version;
    uint16_t checksum;
} calibration_data_t;

// Beat Detection Result
typedef struct {
    bool detected;
    uint32_t timestamp;
    float instant_bpm;
} beat_result_t;

// Global Variables
hw_timer_t *timer = NULL;
volatile bool acquisition_flag = false;
volatile uint32_t isr_timestamp = 0;

// Timing Monitoring
uint32_t last_processing_start = 0;
uint32_t max_observed_processing_time = 0;
uint32_t missed_deadlines = 0;
uint32_t consecutive_misses = 0;

// Signal Processing Buffers
uint32_t red_buffer[FIFO_DEPTH];
uint32_t ir_buffer[FIFO_DEPTH];
int32_t dc_ir = 0;
float sqi = 0.0f;

// Digital Filters
typedef struct {
    float alpha_hp;
    float prev_input;
    float prev_output;
} highpass_filter_t;

typedef struct {
    float alpha_lp;
    float prev_output;
} lowpass_filter_t;

highpass_filter_t hp_filter;
lowpass_filter_t lp_filter;

// Beat Detection Variables
int32_t peak_value = 0;
int32_t valley_value = 0x7FFFFFFF;
int32_t threshold = 0;
uint32_t last_beat_time = 0;
uint32_t beat_intervals[8] = {0};
uint8_t beat_index = 0;
float instant_bpm = 0;
float averaged_bpm = 0;
float smoothed_bpm = 0;

// Adaptive LED Control
uint8_t led_current_reg = 0x14;
uint32_t last_led_adjust_time = 0;

// FIFO Management
uint8_t last_wr_ptr = 0;
uint8_t last_rd_ptr = 0;
uint8_t overflow_counter = 0;
bool fifo_overflow_detected = false;

// Hysteresis Control (Textbook Implementation)
float reference_bpm = 0;
float candidate_bpm = 0;
uint32_t candidate_start_time = 0;
bool candidate_valid = false;

// System State
system_state_t current_state = STATE_INIT;
system_state_t previous_state = STATE_INIT;

// Calibration Data
calibration_data_t calibration = {0, 1, 0};

// Timer Interrupt Service Routine
void IRAM_ATTR onTimer() {
    isr_timestamp = micros();
    acquisition_flag = true;
}

// MAX30105 Register Access Functions
uint8_t readRegister(uint8_t reg) {
    Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
    Wire.write(reg);
    Wire.endTransmission(false);
    Wire.requestFrom(0x57, 1);
    return Wire.available() ? Wire.read() : 0;
}

void writeRegister(uint8_t reg, uint8_t value) {
    Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
    Wire.write(reg);
    Wire.write(value);
    Wire.endTransmission();
}

// FIFO Management with Proper Pointer Writeback
uint8_t getFIFOData(uint32_t* red_data, uint32_t* ir_data, uint8_t max_samples) {
    uint8_t wr_ptr = readRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR);
    uint8_t rd_ptr = readRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR);
    uint8_t ovf_counter = readRegister(REG_OVF_COUNTER);
    
    // Check for overflow
    if (ovf_counter != overflow_counter) {
        fifo_overflow_detected = true;
        overflow_counter = ovf_counter;
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Calculate available samples
    uint8_t samples_available = (wr_ptr >= rd_ptr) ? 
                               (wr_ptr - rd_ptr) : 
                               (32 - rd_ptr + wr_ptr);
    
    uint8_t samples_to_read = min(samples_available, max_samples);
    
    // Read FIFO data
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < samples_to_read; i++) {
        Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
        Wire.write(REG_FIFO_DATA);
        Wire.endTransmission(false);
        Wire.requestFrom(0x57, 6);
        
        if (Wire.available() >= 6) {
            red_data[i] = ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 16) | 
                          ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 8) | 
                          (uint32_t)Wire.read();
            red_data[i] &= 0x03FFFF;
            
            ir_data[i] = ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 16) | 
                         ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 8) | 
                         (uint32_t)Wire.read();
            ir_data[i] &= 0x03FFFF;
        }
    }
    
    // CRITICAL FIX: Write back read pointer
    uint8_t new_rd_ptr = (rd_ptr + samples_to_read) % 32;
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, new_rd_ptr);
    
    last_wr_ptr = wr_ptr;
    last_rd_ptr = new_rd_ptr;
    
    return samples_to_read;
}

// Sensor Reset Without Delay
bool reset_sensor() {
    writeRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG, 0x40); // Reset command
    
    // Poll for reset completion instead of delay
    uint32_t start_time = millis();
    while ((millis() - start_time) < SENSOR_RESET_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        uint8_t mode_reg = readRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG);
        if ((mode_reg & 0x40) == 0) { // Reset bit cleared
            return true;
        }
        delayMicroseconds(100); // Minimal yield
    }
    
    return false; // Timeout
}

// Setup Function
void setup() {
    Serial.begin(115200);
    while (!Serial);
    
    // Initialize EEPROM for calibration
    EEPROM.begin(sizeof(calibration_data_t));
    load_calibration();
    
    // Initialize I2C
    Wire.begin(18, 19);
    Wire.setClock(400000);
    
    // Initialize MAX30105 without delay()
    if (!initialize_sensor()) {
        Serial.println("MAX30105 initialization failed!");
        current_state = STATE_ERROR;
        return;
    }
    
    // Initialize digital filters
    init_filters();
    
    // Initialize timer for deterministic acquisition
    timer = timerBegin(0, 80, true);
    timerAttachInterrupt(timer, &onTimer, true);
    timerAlarmWrite(timer, ACQ_INTERVAL_US, true);
    timerAlarmEnable(timer);
    
    // Initialize buffers
    memset(red_buffer, 0, sizeof(red_buffer));
    memset(ir_buffer, 0, sizeof(ir_buffer));
    memset(beat_intervals, 0, sizeof(beat_intervals));
    
    current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
    Serial.println("~HRM:INIT COMPLETE");
}

// Sensor Initialization
bool initialize_sensor() {
    uint8_t part_id = readRegister(REG_PART_ID);
    if (part_id != 0x15) {
        return false;
    }
    
    // Reset sensor without delay
    if (!reset_sensor()) {
        return false;
    }
    
    // Configure sensor
    writeRegister(REG_INTR_STATUS_1, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_INTR_STATUS_2, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_CONFIG, 0x0F);
    writeRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG, 0x03);
    writeRegister(REG_SPO2_CONFIG, 0x27);
    writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg);
    writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg);
    
    // Clear FIFO properly
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_OVF_COUNTER, 0x00);
    
    return true;
}

// Filter Initialization
void init_filters() {
    float rc_hp = 1.0f / (2.0f * 3.14159f * 0.5f);
    hp_filter.alpha_hp = rc_hp / (rc_hp + (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ));
    hp_filter.prev_input = 0;
    hp_filter.prev_output = 0;
    
    float rc_lp = 1.0f / (2.0f * 3.14159f * 5.0f);
    lp_filter.alpha_lp = (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ) / (rc_lp + (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ));
    lp_filter.prev_output = 0;
}

// Filter Implementations
float apply_highpass_filter(float input) {
    float output = hp_filter.alpha_hp * (hp_filter.prev_output + input - hp_filter.prev_input);
    hp_filter.prev_input = input;
    hp_filter.prev_output = output;
    return output;
}

float apply_lowpass_filter(float input) {
    float output = lp_filter.prev_output + lp_filter.alpha_lp * (input - lp_filter.prev_output);
    lp_filter.prev_output = output;
    return output;
}

// Main Loop
void loop() {
    if (acquisition_flag) {
        acquisition_flag = false;
        
        uint32_t processing_start = micros();
        
        process_acquisition();
        update_state_machine();
        handle_output();
        
        // Deterministic timing monitoring
        uint32_t processing_time = micros() - processing_start;
        if (processing_time > max_observed_processing_time) {
            max_observed_processing_time = processing_time;
        }
        
        uint32_t next_expected = isr_timestamp + ACQ_INTERVAL_US;
        uint32_t now = micros();
        
        if (now > next_expected + 1000) {
            missed_deadlines++;
            consecutive_misses++;
            
            // CRITICAL FIX: Fail-safe on persistent timing violations
            if (consecutive_misses >= MAX_CONSECUTIVE_MISSES) {
                current_state = STATE_ERROR;
                Serial.println("~HRM:FATAL_TIMING_VIOLATION");
            }
        } else {
            consecutive_misses = 0; // Reset on success
        }
    }
}

// Process one acquisition cycle
void process_acquisition() {
    if (fifo_overflow_detected) {
        writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, 0x00);
        writeRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR, 0x00);
        fifo_overflow_detected = false;
        return;
    }
    
    uint8_t num_samples = getFIFOData(red_buffer, ir_buffer, FIFO_DEPTH);
    
    if (num_samples == 0) return;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < num_samples; i++) {
        if (i == 0) {
            dc_ir = ir_buffer[i];
            adjust_led_current();
        }
        
        float filtered_signal = apply_highpass_filter((float)ir_buffer[i]);
        filtered_signal = apply_lowpass_filter(filtered_signal);
        
        beat_result_t beat = detect_beat((int32_t)filtered_signal, millis());
        if (beat.detected) {
            instant_bpm = beat.instant_bpm;
            uint32_t beat_interval = beat.timestamp - last_beat_time;
            beat_intervals[beat_index] = beat_interval;
            beat_index = (beat_index + 1) % 8;
            last_beat_time = beat.timestamp;
        }
    }
    
    calculate_sqi(num_samples);
    
    if (instant_bpm > 0) {
        calculate_bpm();
    }
}

// Adaptive LED Current Control
void adjust_led_current() {
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    if ((current_time - last_led_adjust_time) < LED_SETTLING_TIME_MS) {
        return;
    }
    
    bool adjusted = false;
    
    if (dc_ir < (DC_TARGET_CENTER - DC_TARGET_WINDOW)) {
        if (led_current_reg < LED_CURRENT_MAX) {
            led_current_reg += LED_CURRENT_STEP;
            writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg);
            writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg);
            adjusted = true;
        }
    } else if (dc_ir > (DC_TARGET_CENTER + DC_TARGET_WINDOW)) {
        if (led_current_reg > LED_CURRENT_STEP) {
            led_current_reg -= LED_CURRENT_STEP;
            writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg);
            writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg);
            adjusted = true;
        }
    }
    
    if (adjusted) {
        last_led_adjust_time = current_time;
    }
}

// Accurate Signal Quality Index Calculation
void calculate_sqi(uint8_t num_samples) {
    if (num_samples == 0) {
        sqi = 0.0f;
        return;
    }
    
    // Contact quality
    float contact_score = 0.0f;
    if (dc_ir >= DC_TARGET_MIN && dc_ir <= DC_TARGET_MAX) {
        contact_score = 1.0f;
    } else {
        sqi = 0.0f;
        return;
    }
    
    // Perfusion index
    int32_t min_dc = 0x7FFFFFFF;
    int32_t max_dc = 0;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < min(num_samples, (uint8_t)8); i++) {
        if (ir_buffer[i] < min_dc) min_dc = ir_buffer[i];
        if (ir_buffer[i] > max_dc) max_dc = ir_buffer[i];
    }
    
    int32_t ac_component = max_dc - min_dc;
    float perfusion_ratio = (float)ac_component / (float)dc_ir;
    float perfusion_score = min(1.0f, perfusion_ratio * 10.0f);
    
    // Amplitude adequacy
    float amplitude_score = min(1.0f, (float)ac_component / 1000.0f);
    
    // CRITICAL FIX: Validated interval counting for stability SQI
    float stability_score = 1.0f;
    uint8_t valid_intervals = 0;
    uint32_t valid_sum = 0;
    
    // Count valid intervals
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (beat_intervals[i] > REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS && beat_intervals[i] < 3000) {
            valid_intervals++;
            valid_sum += beat_intervals[i];
        }
    }
    
    // Only compute variance when we have sufficient data
    if (valid_intervals >= 3) {
        float mean_interval = (float)valid_sum / (float)valid_intervals;
        float variance = 0;
        uint8_t variance_count = 0;
        
        for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            if (beat_intervals[i] > REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS && beat_intervals[i] < 3000) {
                float diff = (float)beat_intervals[i] - mean_interval;
                variance += diff * diff;
                variance_count++;
            }
        }
        
        if (variance_count > 0) {
            variance /= (float)variance_count;
            float std_dev = sqrt(variance);
            stability_score = max(0.0f, 1.0f - (std_dev / 200.0f));
        }
    }
    
    // Combine metrics
    sqi = (contact_score * 0.3f) + 
          (perfusion_score * 0.3f) + 
          (amplitude_score * 0.2f) + 
          (stability_score * 0.2f);
    sqi = max(0.0f, min(1.0f, sqi));
}

// Proper Beat Detection with Local Amplitude Context
beat_result_t detect_beat(int32_t sample, uint32_t timestamp) {
    beat_result_t result = {false, 0, 0.0f};
    
    if (sample > peak_value) {
        peak_value = sample;
    }
    if (sample < valley_value) {
        valley_value = sample;
    }
    
    static uint32_t last_threshold_update = 0;
    if ((timestamp - last_threshold_update) > 100) {
        threshold = valley_value + (int32_t)((peak_value - valley_value) * PEAK_THRESHOLD_FACTOR);
        last_threshold_update = timestamp;
    }
    
    static bool above_threshold = false;
    static uint32_t refractory_end = 0;
    
    if (timestamp > refractory_end) {
        if (!above_threshold && sample > threshold) {
            above_threshold = true;
            
            // CRITICAL FIX: Local amplitude validation
            int32_t local_amplitude = peak_value - valley_value;
            int32_t recent_min = 0x7FFFFFFF;
            int32_t recent_max = 0;
            
            // Get recent amplitude context
            for (int i = max(0, (int)beat_index - 3); i < beat_index; i++) {
                if (beat_intervals[i] > 0) {
                    recent_min = min(recent_min, (int32_t)beat_intervals[i]);
                    recent_max = max(recent_max, (int32_t)beat_intervals[i]);
                }
            }
            
            int32_t expected_floor = (recent_max > recent_min) ? 
                                   ((recent_max - recent_min) / 4) : 50;
            
            // Validate against local context
            if (local_amplitude > expected_floor) {
                uint32_t beat_interval = timestamp - last_beat_time;
                
                if (beat_interval >= REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS && beat_interval <= 3000) {
                    result.detected = true;
                    result.timestamp = timestamp;
                    result.instant_bpm = 60000.0f / (float)beat_interval;
                    result.instant_bpm += calibration.bpm_offset;
                    result.instant_bpm = max((float)BPM_MIN, min((float)BPM_MAX, result.instant_bpm));
                    
                    // Reset for next detection
                    peak_value = sample;
                    valley_value = sample;
                }
            }
        } else if (above_threshold && sample < threshold) {
            above_threshold = false;
            peak_value = sample;
            valley_value = sample;
        }
    }
    
    return result;
}

// BPM Averaging and Smoothing
void calculate_bpm() {
    uint32_t sum_intervals = 0;
    uint8_t valid_count = 0;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (beat_intervals[i] > 0) {
            sum_intervals += beat_intervals[i];
            valid_count++;
        }
    }
    
    if (valid_count > 0) {
        float avg_interval = (float)sum_intervals / (float)valid_count;
        averaged_bpm = 60000.0f / avg_interval;
        averaged_bpm += calibration.bpm_offset;
        averaged_bpm = max((float)BPM_MIN, min((float)BPM_MAX, averaged_bpm));
        
        static bool first_bpm = true;
        if (first_bpm) {
            smoothed_bpm = averaged_bpm;
            first_bpm = false;
        } else {
            smoothed_bpm = (smoothed_bpm * 0.8f) + (averaged_bpm * 0.2f);
        }
    }
}

// Textbook Hysteresis Implementation
bool apply_hysteresis(float new_bpm) {
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    if (!candidate_valid) {
        // Establish candidate
        candidate_bpm = new_bpm;
        candidate_start_time = current_time;
        candidate_valid = true;
        return false; // Not locked yet
    }
    
    // Check if candidate is still valid
    if (fabs(new_bpm - candidate_bpm) <= HYSTERESIS_BAND) {
        // Within band - check stability duration
        if ((current_time - candidate_start_time) >= (HYSTERESIS_TIME_SEC * 1000)) {
            // Candidate has been stable - promote to reference
            reference_bpm = candidate_bpm;
            candidate_valid = false; // Ready for next candidate
            return true;
        }
        return false; // Still evaluating candidate
    } else {
        // Out of band - abandon candidate and start new one
        candidate_bpm = new_bpm;
        candidate_start_time = current_time;
        return false;
    }
}

// State Machine Update
void update_state_machine() {
    previous_state = current_state;
    
    switch (current_state) {
        case STATE_INIT:
            current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            break;
            
        case STATE_NO_CONTACT:
            if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_ACQUIRING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ACQUIRING:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.8f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (instant_bpm > 0) {
                current_state = STATE_TRACKING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_TRACKING:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.5f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (sqi < SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_UNSTABLE;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_UNSTABLE:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.5f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_TRACKING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ERROR:
            break;
    }
}

// Output Handling
void handle_output() {
    static uint32_t last_output_time = 0;
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    if ((current_time - last_output_time) < 1000) return;
    last_output_time = current_time;
    
    switch (current_state) {
        case STATE_TRACKING:
            if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD && smoothed_bpm >= BPM_MIN && smoothed_bpm <= BPM_MAX) {
                if (apply_hysteresis(smoothed_bpm)) {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:%.0f,SQI:%.2f\n", smoothed_bpm, sqi);
                } else if (reference_bpm > 0) {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:%.0f,SQI:%.2f\n", reference_bpm, sqi);
                } else {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:WAIT,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
                }
            } else {
                Serial.printf("~HR:N/A,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ACQUIRING:
            Serial.printf("~HR:ACQ,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_UNSTABLE:
            Serial.printf("~HR:UNST,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_NO_CONTACT:
            Serial.printf("~HR:NOCON,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_ERROR:
            Serial.printf("~HR:ERR,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        default:
            Serial.printf("~HR:INIT,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
    }
}

// Calibration Functions
void load_calibration() {
    EEPROM.get(0, calibration);
    
    uint16_t calc_checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
    if (calc_checksum != calibration.checksum) {
        calibration.bpm_offset = 0;
        calibration.version = 1;
        calibration.checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
        save_calibration();
    }
}

void save_calibration() {
    calibration.checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
    EEPROM.put(0, calibration);
    EEPROM.commit();
}

void set_calibration_offset(int16_t offset) {
    calibration.bpm_offset = offset;
    save_calibration();
}
