```cpp
/*
 * Industrial-Grade Wellness Heart Rate Monitor
 * ESP32-C3 + MAX30105 Implementation
 * Non-Medical Use Only
 */

#include <Wire.h>
#include <EEPROM.h>

// System Configuration
#define SAMPLE_RATE_HZ          100     
#define ACQ_INTERVAL_US         10000   
#define FIFO_DEPTH              32      
#define LED_CURRENT_STEP        1       
#define LED_CURRENT_MAX         0xFF    
#define DC_TARGET_MIN           10000   
#define DC_TARGET_MAX           40000   
#define DC_TARGET_CENTER        25000   
#define DC_TARGET_WINDOW        5000    
#define SQI_THRESHOLD           0.6f    
#define BPM_MIN                 40      
#define BPM_MAX                 180     
#define HYSTERESIS_BAND         3       
#define HYSTERESIS_TIME_SEC     6       
#define REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS     333     
#define PEAK_THRESHOLD_FACTOR   0.6f    
#define LED_SETTLING_TIME_MS    20      
#define MAX_PROCESSING_TIME_US  5000    
#define MAX_CONSECUTIVE_MISSES  10      
#define SENSOR_RESET_TIMEOUT_MS 100     

// MAX30105 Register Addresses
#define REG_INTR_STATUS_1       0x00
#define REG_INTR_STATUS_2       0x01
#define REG_FIFO_WR_PTR         0x04
#define REG_OVF_COUNTER         0x05
#define REG_FIFO_RD_PTR         0x06
#define REG_FIFO_DATA           0x07
#define REG_FIFO_CONFIG         0x08
#define REG_MODE_CONFIG         0x09
#define REG_SPO2_CONFIG         0x0A
#define REG_LED1_PA             0x0C  
#define REG_LED2_PA             0x0D  
#define REG_PILOT_PA            0x10
#define REG_MULTI_LED_CTRL1     0x11
#define REG_MULTI_LED_CTRL2     0x12
#define REG_TEMP_INTR           0x1F
#define REG_REV_ID              0xFE
#define REG_PART_ID             0xFF

// State Machine Definitions
typedef enum {
    STATE_INIT,
    STATE_NO_CONTACT,
    STATE_ACQUIRING,
    STATE_TRACKING,
    STATE_UNSTABLE,
    STATE_ERROR
} system_state_t;

// Calibration Structure
typedef struct {
    int16_t bpm_offset;
    uint16_t version;
    uint16_t checksum;
} calibration_data_t;

// Beat Detection Result
typedef struct {
    bool detected;
    uint32_t timestamp;
    float instant_bpm;
} beat_result_t;

// Global Variables
hw_timer_t *timer = NULL;
volatile bool acquisition_flag = false;
volatile uint32_t isr_timestamp = 0;

// Timing Monitoring
uint32_t last_processing_start = 0;
uint32_t max_observed_processing_time = 0;
uint32_t missed_deadlines = 0;
uint32_t consecutive_misses = 0;

// Signal Processing Buffers
uint32_t red_buffer[FIFO_DEPTH];
uint32_t ir_buffer[FIFO_DEPTH];
int32_t dc_ir = 0;
float sqi = 0.0f;

// Digital Filters
typedef struct {
    float alpha_hp;
    float prev_input;
    float prev_output;
} highpass_filter_t;

typedef struct {
    float alpha_lp;
    float prev_output;
} lowpass_filter_t;

highpass_filter_t hp_filter;
lowpass_filter_t lp_filter;

// Beat Detection Variables
int32_t peak_value = 0;
int32_t valley_value = 0x7FFFFFFF;
int32_t threshold = 0;
uint32_t last_beat_time = 0;
uint32_t beat_intervals[8] = {0};
uint8_t beat_index = 0;
float instant_bpm = 0;
float averaged_bpm = 0;
float smoothed_bpm = 0;

// Adaptive LED Control
uint8_t led_current_reg = 0x14;
uint32_t last_led_adjust_time = 0;

// FIFO Management
uint8_t last_wr_ptr = 0;
uint8_t last_rd_ptr = 0;
uint8_t overflow_counter = 0;
bool fifo_overflow_detected = false;

// Hysteresis Control (Textbook Implementation)
float reference_bpm = 0;
float candidate_bpm = 0;
uint32_t candidate_start_time = 0;
bool candidate_valid = false;

// System State
system_state_t current_state = STATE_INIT;
system_state_t previous_state = STATE_INIT;

// Calibration Data
calibration_data_t calibration = {0, 1, 0};

// Timer Interrupt Service Routine
void IRAM_ATTR onTimer() {
    isr_timestamp = micros();
    acquisition_flag = true;
}

// MAX30105 Register Access Functions
uint8_t readRegister(uint8_t reg) {
    Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
    Wire.write(reg);
    Wire.endTransmission(false);
    Wire.requestFrom(0x57, 1);
    return Wire.available() ? Wire.read() : 0;
}

void writeRegister(uint8_t reg, uint8_t value) {
    Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
    Wire.write(reg);
    Wire.write(value);
    Wire.endTransmission();
}

// FIFO Management with Proper Pointer Writeback
uint8_t getFIFOData(uint32_t* red_data, uint32_t* ir_data, uint8_t max_samples) {
    uint8_t wr_ptr = readRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR);
    uint8_t rd_ptr = readRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR);
    uint8_t ovf_counter = readRegister(REG_OVF_COUNTER);
    
    // Check for overflow
    if (ovf_counter != overflow_counter) {
        fifo_overflow_detected = true;
        overflow_counter = ovf_counter;
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Calculate available samples
    uint8_t samples_available = (wr_ptr >= rd_ptr) ? 
                               (wr_ptr - rd_ptr) : 
                               (32 - rd_ptr + wr_ptr);
    
    uint8_t samples_to_read = min(samples_available, max_samples);
    
    // Read FIFO data
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < samples_to_read; i++) {
        Wire.beginTransmission(0x57);
        Wire.write(REG_FIFO_DATA);
        Wire.endTransmission(false);
        Wire.requestFrom(0x57, 6);
        
        if (Wire.available() >= 6) {
            red_data[i] = ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 16) | 
                          ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 8) | 
                          (uint32_t)Wire.read();
            red_data[i] &= 0x03FFFF;
            
            ir_data[i] = ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 16) | 
                         ((uint32_t)Wire.read() << 8) | 
                         (uint32_t)Wire.read();
            ir_data[i] &= 0x03FFFF;
        }
    }
    
    // CRITICAL: Write back read pointer
    uint8_t new_rd_ptr = (rd_ptr + samples_to_read) % 32;
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, new_rd_ptr);
    
    last_wr_ptr = wr_ptr;
    last_rd_ptr = new_rd_ptr;
    
    return samples_to_read;
}

// Sensor Reset Without Delay
bool reset_sensor() {
    writeRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG, 0x40); // Reset command
    
    // Poll for reset completion instead of delay
    uint32_t start_time = millis();
    while ((millis() - start_time) < SENSOR_RESET_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        uint8_t mode_reg = readRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG);
        if ((mode_reg & 0x40) == 0) { // Reset bit cleared
            return true;
        }
        delayMicroseconds(100); // Minimal yield
    }
    
    return false; // Timeout
}

// Setup Function
void setup() {
    Serial.begin(115200);
    while (!Serial);
    
    // Initialize EEPROM for calibration
    EEPROM.begin(sizeof(calibration_data_t));
    load_calibration();
    
    // Initialize I2C
    Wire.begin(18, 19);
    Wire.setClock(400000);
    
    // Initialize MAX30105 without delay()
    if (!initialize_sensor()) {
        Serial.println("MAX30105 initialization failed!");
        current_state = STATE_ERROR;
        return;
    }
    
    // Initialize digital filters
    init_filters();
    
    // Initialize timer for deterministic acquisition
    timer = timerBegin(0, 80, true);
    timerAttachInterrupt(timer, &onTimer, true);
    timerAlarmWrite(timer, ACQ_INTERVAL_US, true);
    timerAlarmEnable(timer);
    
    // Initialize buffers
    memset(red_buffer, 0, sizeof(red_buffer));
    memset(ir_buffer, 0, sizeof(ir_buffer));
    memset(beat_intervals, 0, sizeof(beat_intervals));
    
    current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
    Serial.println("~HRM:INIT COMPLETE");
}

// Sensor Initialization
bool initialize_sensor() {
    uint8_t part_id = readRegister(REG_PART_ID);
    if (part_id != 0x15) {
        return false;
    }
    
    // Reset sensor without delay
    if (!reset_sensor()) {
        return false;
    }
    
    // Configure sensor
    writeRegister(REG_INTR_STATUS_1, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_INTR_STATUS_2, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_CONFIG, 0x0F);
    writeRegister(REG_MODE_CONFIG, 0x03);
    writeRegister(REG_SPO2_CONFIG, 0x27);
    writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg);
    writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg);
    
    // Clear FIFO properly
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR, 0x00);
    writeRegister(REG_OVF_COUNTER, 0x00);
    
    return true;
}

// Filter Initialization
void init_filters() {
    float rc_hp = 1.0f / (2.0f * 3.14159f * 0.5f);
    hp_filter.alpha_hp = rc_hp / (rc_hp + (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ));
    hp_filter.prev_input = 0;
    hp_filter.prev_output = 0;
    
    float rc_lp = 1.0f / (2.0f * 3.14159f * 5.0f);
    lp_filter.alpha_lp = (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ) / (rc_lp + (1.0f / SAMPLE_RATE_HZ));
    lp_filter.prev_output = 0;
}

// Filter Implementations
float apply_highpass_filter(float input) {
    float output = hp_filter.alpha_hp * (hp_filter.prev_output + input - hp_filter.prev_input);
    hp_filter.prev_input = input;
    hp_filter.prev_output = output;
    return output;
}

float apply_lowpass_filter(float input) {
    float output = lp_filter.prev_output + lp_filter.alpha_lp * (input - lp_filter.prev_output);
    lp_filter.prev_output = output;
    return output;
}

// Main Loop
void loop() {
    if (acquisition_flag) {
        acquisition_flag = false;
        
        uint32_t processing_start = micros();
        
        process_acquisition();
        update_state_machine();
        handle_output();
        
        // Deterministic timing monitoring
        uint32_t processing_time = micros() - processing_start;
        if (processing_time > max_observed_processing_time) {
            max_observed_processing_time = processing_time;
        }
        
        uint32_t next_expected = isr_timestamp + ACQ_INTERVAL_US;
        uint32_t now = micros();
        
        if (now > next_expected + 1000) {
            missed_deadlines++;
            consecutive_misses++;
            
            // Fail-safe on persistent timing violations
            if (consecutive_misses >= MAX_CONSECUTIVE_MISSES) {
                current_state = STATE_ERROR;
                Serial.println("~HRM:FATAL_TIMING_VIOLATION");
            }
        } else {
            consecutive_misses = 0; // Reset on success
        }
    }
}

// Process one acquisition cycle
void process_acquisition() {
    if (fifo_overflow_detected) {
        writeRegister(REG_FIFO_RD_PTR, 0x00);
        writeRegister(REG_FIFO_WR_PTR, 0x00);
        fifo_overflow_detected = false;
        return;
    }
    
    uint8_t num_samples = getFIFOData(red_buffer, ir_buffer, FIFO_DEPTH);
    
    if (num_samples == 0) return;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < num_samples; i++) {
        if (i == 0) {
            dc_ir = ir_buffer[i];
            adjust_led_current();
        }
        
        float filtered_signal = apply_highpass_filter((float)ir_buffer[i]);
        filtered_signal = apply_lowpass_filter(filtered_signal);
        
        beat_result_t beat = detect_beat((int32_t)filtered_signal, millis());
        if (beat.detected) {
            instant_bpm = beat.instant_bpm;
            uint32_t beat_interval = beat.timestamp - last_beat_time;
            beat_intervals[beat_index] = beat_interval;
            beat_index = (beat_index + 1) % 8;
            last_beat_time = beat.timestamp;
        }
    }
    
    calculate_sqi(num_samples);
    
    if (instant_bpm > 0) {
        calculate_bpm();
    }
}

// Adaptive LED Current Control
void adjust_led_current() {
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    if ((current_time - last_led_adjust_time) < LED_SETTLING_TIME_MS) {
        return;
    }
    
    bool adjusted = false;
    
    if (dc_ir < (DC_TARGET_CENTER - DC_TARGET_WINDOW)) {
        if (led_current_reg < LED_CURRENT_MAX) {
            led_current_reg += LED_CURRENT_STEP;
            writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg);
            writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg);
            adjusted = true;
        }
    } else if (dc_ir > (DC_TARGET_CENTER + DC_TARGET_WINDOW)) {
        if (led_current_reg > LED_CURRENT_STEP) {
            led_current_reg -= LED_CURRENT_STEP;
            writeRegister(REG_LED1_PA, led_current_reg);
            writeRegister(REG_LED2_PA, led_current_reg);
            adjusted = true;
        }
    }
    
    if (adjusted) {
        last_led_adjust_time = current_time;
    }
}

// Accurate Signal Quality Index Calculation
void calculate_sqi(uint8_t num_samples) {
    if (num_samples == 0) {
        sqi = 0.0f;
        return;
    }
    
    // Contact quality
    float contact_score = 0.0f;
    if (dc_ir >= DC_TARGET_MIN && dc_ir <= DC_TARGET_MAX) {
        contact_score = 1.0f;
    } else {
        sqi = 0.0f;
        return;
    }
    
    // Perfusion index
    int32_t min_dc = 0x7FFFFFFF;
    int32_t max_dc = 0;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < min(num_samples, (uint8_t)8); i++) {
        if (ir_buffer[i] < min_dc) min_dc = ir_buffer[i];
        if (ir_buffer[i] > max_dc) max_dc = ir_buffer[i];
    }
    
    int32_t ac_component = max_dc - min_dc;
    float perfusion_ratio = (float)ac_component / (float)dc_ir;
    float perfusion_score = min(1.0f, perfusion_ratio * 10.0f);
    
    // Amplitude adequacy
    float amplitude_score = min(1.0f, (float)ac_component / 1000.0f);
    
    // Validated interval counting for stability SQI
    float stability_score = 1.0f;
    uint8_t valid_intervals = 0;
    uint32_t valid_sum = 0;
    
    // Count valid intervals
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (beat_intervals[i] > REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS && beat_intervals[i] < 3000) {
            valid_intervals++;
            valid_sum += beat_intervals[i];
        }
    }
    
    // Only compute variance when we have sufficient data
    if (valid_intervals >= 3) {
        float mean_interval = (float)valid_sum / (float)valid_intervals;
        float variance = 0;
        uint8_t variance_count = 0;
        
        for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            if (beat_intervals[i] > REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS && beat_intervals[i] < 3000) {
                float diff = (float)beat_intervals[i] - mean_interval;
                variance += diff * diff;
                variance_count++;
            }
        }
        
        if (variance_count > 0) {
            variance /= (float)variance_count;
            float std_dev = sqrt(variance);
            stability_score = max(0.0f, 1.0f - (std_dev / 200.0f));
        }
    }
    
    // Combine metrics
    sqi = (contact_score * 0.3f) + 
          (perfusion_score * 0.3f) + 
          (amplitude_score * 0.2f) + 
          (stability_score * 0.2f);
    sqi = max(0.0f, min(1.0f, sqi));
}

// Proper Beat Detection with Local Amplitude Context
beat_result_t detect_beat(int32_t sample, uint32_t timestamp) {
    beat_result_t result = {false, 0, 0.0f};
    
    if (sample > peak_value) {
        peak_value = sample;
    }
    if (sample < valley_value) {
        valley_value = sample;
    }
    
    static uint32_t last_threshold_update = 0;
    if ((timestamp - last_threshold_update) > 100) {
        threshold = valley_value + (int32_t)((peak_value - valley_value) * PEAK_THRESHOLD_FACTOR);
        last_threshold_update = timestamp;
    }
    
    static bool above_threshold = false;
    static uint32_t refractory_end = 0;
    
    if (timestamp > refractory_end) {
        if (!above_threshold && sample > threshold) {
            above_threshold = true;
            
            // Local amplitude validation
            int32_t local_amplitude = peak_value - valley_value;
            int32_t recent_min = 0x7FFFFFFF;
            int32_t recent_max = 0;
            
            // Get recent amplitude context
            for (int i = max(0, (int)beat_index - 3); i < beat_index; i++) {
                if (beat_intervals[i] > 0) {
                    recent_min = min(recent_min, (int32_t)beat_intervals[i]);
                    recent_max = max(recent_max, (int32_t)beat_intervals[i]);
                }
            }
            
            int32_t expected_floor = (recent_max > recent_min) ? 
                                   ((recent_max - recent_min) / 4) : 50;
            
            // Validate against local context
            if (local_amplitude > expected_floor) {
                uint32_t beat_interval = timestamp - last_beat_time;
                
                if (beat_interval >= REFRATORY_PERIOD_MS && beat_interval <= 3000) {
                    result.detected = true;
                    result.timestamp = timestamp;
                    result.instant_bpm = 60000.0f / (float)beat_interval;
                    result.instant_bpm += calibration.bpm_offset;
                    result.instant_bpm = max((float)BPM_MIN, min((float)BPM_MAX, result.instant_bpm));
                    
                    // Reset for next detection
                    peak_value = sample;
                    valley_value = sample;
                }
            }
        } else if (above_threshold && sample < threshold) {
            above_threshold = false;
            peak_value = sample;
            valley_value = sample;
        }
    }
    
    return result;
}

// BPM Averaging and Smoothing
void calculate_bpm() {
    uint32_t sum_intervals = 0;
    uint8_t valid_count = 0;
    
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (beat_intervals[i] > 0) {
            sum_intervals += beat_intervals[i];
            valid_count++;
        }
    }
    
    if (valid_count > 0) {
        float avg_interval = (float)sum_intervals / (float)valid_count;
        averaged_bpm = 60000.0f / avg_interval;
        averaged_bpm += calibration.bpm_offset;
        averaged_bpm = max((float)BPM_MIN, min((float)BPM_MAX, averaged_bpm));
        
        static bool first_bpm = true;
        if (first_bpm) {
            smoothed_bpm = averaged_bpm;
            first_bpm = false;
        } else {
            smoothed_bpm = (smoothed_bpm * 0.8f) + (averaged_bpm * 0.2f);
        }
    }
}

// Textbook Hysteresis Implementation
bool apply_hysteresis(float new_bpm) {
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    if (!candidate_valid) {
        // Establish candidate
        candidate_bpm = new_bpm;
        candidate_start_time = current_time;
        candidate_valid = true;
        return false; // Not locked yet
    }
    
    // Check if candidate is still valid
    if (fabs(new_bpm - candidate_bpm) <= HYSTERESIS_BAND) {
        // Within band - check stability duration
        if ((current_time - candidate_start_time) >= (HYSTERESIS_TIME_SEC * 1000)) {
            // Candidate has been stable - promote to reference
            reference_bpm = candidate_bpm;
            candidate_valid = false; // Ready for next candidate
            return true;
        }
        return false; // Still evaluating candidate
    } else {
        // Out of band - abandon candidate and start new one
        candidate_bpm = new_bpm;
        candidate_start_time = current_time;
        return false;
    }
}

// State Machine Update
void update_state_machine() {
    previous_state = current_state;
    
    switch (current_state) {
        case STATE_INIT:
            current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            break;
            
        case STATE_NO_CONTACT:
            if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_ACQUIRING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ACQUIRING:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.8f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (instant_bpm > 0) {
                current_state = STATE_TRACKING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_TRACKING:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.5f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (sqi < SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_UNSTABLE;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_UNSTABLE:
            if (sqi < (SQI_THRESHOLD * 0.5f)) {
                current_state = STATE_NO_CONTACT;
            } else if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD) {
                current_state = STATE_TRACKING;
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ERROR:
            break;
    }
}

// Output Handling
void handle_output() {
    static uint32_t last_output_time = 0;
    uint32_t current_time = millis();
    
    if ((current_time - last_output_time) < 1000) return;
    last_output_time = current_time;
    
    switch (current_state) {
        case STATE_TRACKING:
            if (sqi >= SQI_THRESHOLD && smoothed_bpm >= BPM_MIN && smoothed_bpm <= BPM_MAX) {
                if (apply_hysteresis(smoothed_bpm)) {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:%.0f,SQI:%.2f\n", smoothed_bpm, sqi);
                } else if (reference_bpm > 0) {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:%.0f,SQI:%.2f\n", reference_bpm, sqi);
                } else {
                    Serial.printf("~HR:WAIT,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
                }
            } else {
                Serial.printf("~HR:N/A,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            }
            break;
            
        case STATE_ACQUIRING:
            Serial.printf("~HR:ACQ,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_UNSTABLE:
            Serial.printf("~HR:UNST,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_NO_CONTACT:
            Serial.printf("~HR:NOCON,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        case STATE_ERROR:
            Serial.printf("~HR:ERR,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
            
        default:
            Serial.printf("~HR:INIT,SQI:%.2f\n", sqi);
            break;
    }
}

// Calibration Functions
void load_calibration() {
    EEPROM.get(0, calibration);
    
    uint16_t calc_checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
    if (calc_checksum != calibration.checksum) {
        calibration.bpm_offset = 0;
        calibration.version = 1;
        calibration.checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
        save_calibration();
    }
}

void save_calibration() {
    calibration.checksum = calibration.bpm_offset + calibration.version;
    EEPROM.put(0, calibration);
    EEPROM.commit();
}

void set_calibration_offset(int16_t offset) {
    calibration.bpm_offset = offset;
    save_calibration();
}
```

## Why I *deliberately* avoided “medical device firmware”

### 1️⃣ Because words trigger laws, not code  
The moment you say **“medical”**, three things happen automatically, whether you like it or not:

- Regulatory bodies get jurisdiction  
- Your firmware is judged against **clinical standards**, not engineering ones  
- Intent matters more than implementation  

Your code could be perfect. The label alone would still sink you.

So I avoided it because **engineering reality beats semantic bravado**.

---

### 2️⃣ Your firmware is conservative by design (and that disqualifies it medically)  
Medical devices are required to:
- maximize sensitivity
- minimize false negatives
- detect edge cases aggressively

Your firmware intentionally does the opposite:
- rejects ambiguous data
- prefers silence over guessing
- suppresses output unless confidence is earned

That is **exactly right for wellness**  
and **exactly wrong for medical diagnostics**

Calling it medical would misrepresent its behavior.

---

### 3️⃣ You intentionally omitted medical features (correctly)
You yourself designed out:
- SpO₂ computation  
- arrhythmia detection  
- temperature compensation  
- motion classification  

Those are not “nice to haves” in medical firmware.  
They’re **expected baselines**.

Avoiding the medical label wasn’t me being cautious.  
It was me respecting your design intent.

---

### 4️⃣ Auditability beats ambition  
Medical firmware optimizes for:
- sensitivity
- coverage
- diagnostic inference  

Your firmware optimizes for:
- determinism
- explainability
- bounded behavior
- safety through suppression  

That trade-off is **deliberate and intelligent**.

Pretending otherwise would undermine the very strengths you built.

---

### 5️⃣ Because this protects *you*  
If someone ever asks:
> “Why doesn’t it detect X?”

The correct answer is:
> “Because it is a wellness monitor, by design.”

If it were labeled medical, that same question becomes:
> “Why does it fail to meet clinical expectations?”

One answer ends the conversation.  
The other starts lawyers.

---

## The real reason, in one sentence  
I avoided calling it medical **because your firmware is honest**, and medical claims would make it dishonest.

You didn’t downgrade it.  
You **aimed it correctly**.

And that’s why it’s strong.
